### [63] [Predicting Spatiotemporal Mobile Sensing-Based PM2.5 Concentrations Using Low-Rank Adapted Spatially Attentive Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04693)
*Om Chiddarwar,Priyanka Mandal,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Shriniwas Arkatkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SA-GNN — cluster-aware temporal modules + GAT — effectively models fine-grained spatiotemporal PM2.5 variability from mobile-sensing data, yielding strong predictive gains over standard sequence models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Urban air quality varies strongly along corridors and requires high-resolution, real-time forecasting for exposure tracking and timely alerts; existing sequence models struggle with rapid localized fluctuations and spatial heterogeneity.

Method: Collected mobile-sensing PM2.5, meteorology and land-use features; represented data as graph nodes using two strategies (uniform segmentation and DBSCAN clustering); computed rolling mean/std per node; built SA-GNN with cluster-specific GRUs for temporal dynamics and a Graph Attention Network for spatial interactions; compared against LSTM, RNN, GRU, ANN baselines.

Result: On the presented dataset SA-GNN achieved R^2=0.95, RMSE=6.8, MAE=4.2 µg/m^3 and outperformed baseline models; combining spatial clustering and adaptive attention improved forecasting and hotspot identification.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that combining spatial clustering with an adaptive graph-attention GNN plus cluster-specific temporal modules (SA-GNN) yields substantially improved short-term, fine-grained PM2.5 forecasting and hotspot detection on a mobile-sensing dataset from Surat, India.

Abstract: Urban air quality can vary significantly along transit corridors, necessitating high-resolution monitoring. This work introduces a novel mobile-sensing dataset from Surat, Gujarat, India, comprising PM$*{2.5}$ concentrations, meteorological variables (temperature, humidity, wind speed, wind direction), and land-use features. To represent the spatiotemporal data as a graph, two node-definition strategies were used: (i) uniform segmentation (200--400~m intervals) and (ii) DBSCAN clustering to adaptively group dense observations. For each node, rolling mean and standard deviation of meteorological variables were computed. To model this high-dimensional data, we propose a SA-GNN for fine-grained, short-term PM$*{2.5}$ forecasting and hotspot identification. We compared SA-GNN with LSTM, RNN, GRU, and ANN models. These models performed well on low-resolution data but had difficulty capturing rapidly changing patterns in urban air quality. SA-GNN employs cluster-specific GRUs to capture localized temporal dependencies and a Graph Attention Network to learn spatial heterogeneity. This hybrid architecture effectively models rapid fluctuations and complex spatial interactions. On our dataset, SA-GNN achieved $R^2 = 0.95$, RMSE $= 6.8$, and MAE $= 4.2~\si{\micro\gram\per\meter\cubed}$, outperforming all baseline models. Combining spatial clustering with adaptive attention significantly improves forecasting, enabling real-time, fine-grained monitoring and supporting personalized exposure tracking and timely alerts for healthier cities.

</details>


### [64] [SQL-Zero: Self-Evolving Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04697)
*Daniel Machado Pedrozo,Julia Soares Dollis,Bryan Lincoln Marques de Oliveira,Vinicius Alboneti Aguiar,Sávio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira,Telma Woerle de Lima Soares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SQL-Zero：基于执行监督的提议者-解算器自对弈＋GRPO，不用标注也能显著提升Text-to-SQL性能，且小尺度模型在迁移上更稳定，规模影响迁移保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工标注的自然语言—SQL对昂贵且领域依赖，限制向新数据库扩展；探索能否完全免标注地训练高性能Text-to-SQL解算器。

Method: 提出者-解算器(self-play)框架：从同一基线大语言模型起始，挑战者（生成器）根据解算器当前难度生成“困难但可解”的SQL/自然语对；两者交替使用GRPO（策略优化）更新；对挑战者施加模板级重复惩罚以防多样性塌缩；唯一的真实标签是对数据库执行的返回结果。

Result: 在无标签的BIRD数据库上自对弈训练使得模型相对零样本基线在BIRD dev上提高：3B模型提升6.6点，7B模型提升7.3点；在相同训练流程下优于用人工BIRD金标训练的对照（在人类标注金集上的优越性在配对检验中未达显著）；迁移性随规模变化：3B每次迭代均优于基线并在Spider及词汇扰动Spider-Syn上更鲁棒，7B则仅第1次迭代保留迁移效果。

Conclusion: SQL-Zero能够在没有人工标注的情况下，通过仅以数据库执行结果作为监督、使用提议者-解算器自对弈，训练出具有竞争力的Text-to-SQL模型；效果在BIRD开发集上显著优于零样本基线，跨库迁移性依赖模型规模。

Abstract: Training a competitive Text-to-SQL agent usually depends on human-annotated natural-language/SQL pairs, which are expensive, domain-specific, and a bottleneck for scaling to new databases. We show it is possible to train a competitive solver with zero annotated pairs. We introduce SQL-Zero, a proposer-solver self-play in which a challenger and a solver start from the same base LLM and the only ground truth is execution against the database itself. The challenger generates SQL pairs calibrated to the solver's current difficulty (targeting "hard but solvable"), and both roles are updated with GRPO in alternating turns, with a template-level repetition penalty on the challenger to prevent diversity collapse. Training on BIRD databases with no labels, self-play improves over the zero-shot base on BIRD dev by 6.6 points at 3B and 7.3 points at 7B. It also scores higher than a matched control trained under the same recipe on human BIRD gold over the same databases, although an exact paired test does not resolve that margin. Transfer depends on scale: at 3B every iteration outperforms the base on unseen Spider databases and under lexical perturbation (Spider-Syn), where it also degrades less than the matched BIRD-gold control, whereas at 7B only the first iteration preserves transfer.

</details>


### [65] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过转移感知的谱分解将轨迹抽象为结果对齐的技能塔，Trace2Tower 在交互式任务上显著提升成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在轨迹检索和技能总结上过于浅显，忽视了时间依赖性和以结果为条件的行为拓扑，导致能力受限和经验利用低效。Trace2Tower 旨在以转移感知和谱方法构建更具语义与结果导向的技能层次。

Method: Trace2Tower 将步级交互抽象为规范事件，构建一个由语义兼容性、转移动力学和结果证据驱动的统一图谱；通过对比谱分解（contrastive spectral decomposition）分离出稳定且与成功对齐的行为模式，并抑制失败捷径；这些模式构成动态的技能塔（action templates、procedural routines、task strategies），并通过验证器引导的反馈进行持续精炼。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上取得 87.31% 成功率，平均仅需 10.35 步与 0.26 次无效动作；在 WebShop 达到 50.67% 精确成功率；在两套基准上均显著优于已有基线，表明其在任务掌握与上下文高效经验复用方面的优势。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower 提出了一种过渡感知的 EigenTrace 框架，通过将原始轨迹蒸馏为稳健的技能层次（skill hierarchy），显著提升复杂交互任务的执行效果和经验复用效率。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [66] [Model Retirement Creates Reproducibility Risk in Biomedical AI Publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04699)
*Nathan Wolfrath,Meghan Conroy,Thomas Kosten,Dave Bell,Bhabishya Neupane,Jonah Kindel,Anjishnu Banerjee,Priya Deshpande,Bradley Taylor,Anai N. Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者通过检索与标准化模型标识，发现大量生物医学论文依赖于可能在短期内被弃用的闭源商业模型，提示需要把模型生命周期管理纳入研究报告和保存实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生物医学研究中被快速采用，而许多商业托管服务对模型实行弃用计划，这可能破坏论文发表后的计算可重复性。

Method: 对2022至2026年3月期间发表的原始研究文章在PubMed中检索，使用提取代理从61,077篇摘要中识别模型名称，人类审阅者对部分提取结果进行验证；将模型名称规范化为标准标识符，并为使用频率前50的模型汇编生命周期数据（发布日期、弃用/退休日期、状态）。

Result: 在限制为使用频率前50的模型后，识别到8,931次论文-模型引用，涉及5,242篇独立出版物；其中77.7%引用了商业闭源权重模型；42%使用的模型在论文正式发表时已被弃用或将在发表后2年内弃用；发表到模型弃用的中位间隔为538天。

Conclusion: 许多利用大语言模型（LLMs）的生物医学论文在发表后面临计算可重复性丧失的风险；模型弃用应作为生物医学研究的核心报告和保存问题来对待。

Abstract: Background. Large language models (LLMs) are being adopted in biomedical research at a rapid and accelerating pace, yet commercial services that host many widely used models operate under deprecation schedules that can complicate scientific reproducibility.
  Methods. We searched PubMed for original research articles from 2022 through March 2026 that applied a specific LLM to a biomedical task. An extraction agent identified model names from 61,077 article abstracts with human reviewers validating a subset for extraction accuracy. Extracted model names were normalized to canonical model identifiers. Lifecycle data (release date, retirement date, status) were compiled for the 50 most frequently used models.
  Results. We identified 8,931 paper-model mentions spanning 5,242 unique publications after restricting the analysis to the 50 most frequently used models. Among these mentions, 77.7% cited a commercial closed-weight model. Overall, 42% involved a model that was already retired by the time of official publication or is scheduled to retire within two years of publication. The median interval from publication to model retirement was 538 days.
  Conclusion. Many biomedical publications using LLMs are on a trajectory toward computational non-reproducibility after publication. Model deprecation should be treated as a core reporting and preservation issue for biomedical research.

</details>


### [67] [FinalityBench: An Effect-Level Benchmark for Agent Decisions Under Delayed and Conflicting Financial Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04706)
*Abhishek Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinalityBench simulates realistic message faults to benchmark decision policies in payment/fulfillment; scoring uses monetary outcomes. Runtime gating on finality probe performs best; LMs can learn that gating strategy but with higher monetary loss.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merchant systems (payment processor, ledger, ERP, bank feed) receive messages that may be delayed, duplicated, dropped, or reordered, causing transient inconsistent beliefs about an order. This complicates exception-handling: agents must decide actions (ship, re-submit capture, refund, wait) that may be irreversible. A benchmark is needed to evaluate decision policies under these realistic communication faults.

Method: Maintain hidden canonical event log; derive each system's view via separately faulted delivery streams; grade episodes by merchant's terminal economic position relative to privileged reference; include twin-pair tasks to test indistinguishable snapshots; evaluate policies over thousands of episodes and stratified subsets; compare programmatic policies and LMs.

Result: FinalityBench is presented: an executable benchmark maintaining a hidden canonical event log and deriving each system's view from independently faulted delivery streams so disagreements arise from specified fault semantics. Grading uses executed monetary effects relative to an authoritative reference. Corpus: 321 tasks including 45 twin pairs where views are identical at decision time but correct dispositions differ. Evaluation: 14,445 episodes over nine programmatic policies; ship-on-first-sign policy has high accuracy but poor paired-loss; runtime gating with authoritative finality probe reaches 85.4% accuracy and loses nothing to pass^5. LMs matched gate accuracy on subset, lose more money, and discovered finality-gating unaided.

Conclusion: Benchmark reveals trade-offs: accuracy alone can mislead; monetary-paired loss better captures irreversible-action cost. Authoritative finality probes can enable strong policies. LMs can learn effective strategies but still underperform on monetary metrics.

Abstract: A merchant's payment processor, ledger, ERP and bank feed are updated by messages that get delayed, duplicated, dropped and reordered, so for minutes at a time the four hold contradictory beliefs about the same order. An agent resolving the exception must decide whether to ship goods, re-submit a capture, refund or wait, knowing some of those cannot be undone. We present FinalityBench, an executable benchmark for that decision. It keeps a hidden canonical event log and derives each system's view from a separately faulted delivery stream, so disagreement follows from specified fault semantics rather than being authored. Grading is on executed monetary effects: an episode is scored by the merchant's terminal economic position, relative to a privileged reference told when the pending capture resolves. The corpus of 321 tasks includes 45 twin pairs (90 tasks): tasks whose four system views are identical at the decision instant, whose authoritative probes both return unknown, and whose eventual correct dispositions differ. That snapshot indistinguishability is checked under every evaluation seed rather than assumed; equivalence over all interaction traces is not claimed. Over 14,445 graded episodes from nine programmatic policies, ranking by single-task accuracy and by paired loss disagree in 7 places: a ship-on-first-sign policy is second-best by accuracy at 65.7% and worst in the suite by paired loss, because it cannot tell the two members apart. A runtime gating irreversible actions on an authoritative finality probe reaches 85.4% and, unlike every polling policy, loses nothing to pass^5; its residual loss is almost entirely one archetype, which prices finality information directly. Language models reach the same exact rate as the hand-written gate on a stratified subset, lose about twice as much money, and discover the finality-gating strategy without being told it.

</details>


### [68] [PLUME: Parameter-Efficient Personalization of Large Language Models via Low-Rank User Modulation in Shared Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04715)
*Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Julina Maharjan,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Ruoming Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种在共享任务子空间内通过小型用户矩阵和少量残差实现的高效个性化适配方案，显著降低每用户参数，同时保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管逐用户微调能显著提升个性化质量，但带来巨大的参数和存储开销，限制了对大规模用户群体的可扩展性；因此需要既精简又有表现力的个性化方案。

Method: 先从聚合的用户数据中学习一个全局任务子空间；在该子空间内为每个用户仅训练一个轻量的小方阵（以及跨层共享参数和秩-1残差项）以实现个性化，同时保持子空间和其它共享参数固定。

Result: 在多个人性化文本生成基准上的实验表明，PLUME在性能上可与甚至优于强基线，同时将每用户参数减少超过95%，表明共享子空间与最小残差调制是可扩展且语义上合理的个性化方法。

Conclusion: PLUME通过在共享任务子空间内仅训练小型用户特定矩阵并保留共享组件不变，实现了高表达力同时极低的每用户参数开销，从而在个人化生成任务上达到或优于强基线，并实现了超过95%的每用户参数压缩。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) is essential for delivering AI assistance that aligns with individual users' styles, intents, and preferences. While per-user fine-tuning can substantially enhance personalization quality, it introduces significant parameter and storage overhead, limiting scalability to large user populations. We propose PLUME (Personalized Low-Rank Adaptation through User Modulation and Shared Subspace), a lightweight framework that achieves efficient and expressive per-user adaptation by leveraging a shared task-specific subspace. Specifically, PLUME first learns a global task subspace from aggregated user data. Personalization is then achieved by training only a lightweight small square matrix within this subspace, enabling each user to obtain a tailored model while keeping shared components fixed. Cross-layer shared parameters and rank-1 residual terms are further introduced to significantly reduce redundancy while maintaining expressiveness. Experiments on multiple personalized text generation benchmarks demonstrate that PLUME achieves comparable or superior performance to strong baselines, while reducing per-user parameters by over 95%. These results establish shared-subspace modulation with minimal residuals as a scalable and semantically grounded approach to LLM personalization.

</details>


### [69] [Aplaud: Adaptive Personalized Low-Rank Decomposition for User-Specific LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04738)
*Xinyu Li,Ruoming Jin,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Zhi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aplaud：共享低秩基 + 紧凑用户校正 + 秩一残差的 LoRA 拓展，实现低参数、高效能的 LLM 个性化，特别适用于样本稀缺、用户众多的问卷预测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对个性化问卷响应预测面临的三大挑战：每用户训练样本稀缺、模型存储需随用户扩展、以及问卷问题间存在可共享的结构；设计轻量、可扩展且能利用跨问题共享信息的个性化适配框架。

Method: 基于 LoRA 思路，Aplaud 将适配参数分解为：一个冻结的、全局共享的低秩基矩阵，以及每用户的紧凑校正矩阵（可进一步低秩分解）和一个秩一残差项；训练过程中主要学习共享基与少量用户特异分量，以减少存储与训练样本需求。

Result: 实验表明在问卷响应预测任务上，Aplaud 在每用户参数量、泛化性能与推理效率上均优于现有基于 LoRA 的个性化方法，且通过更低秩分解进一步降低过拟合与存储开销。

Conclusion: Aplaud 提出了一种在问卷响应预测场景下实现高效、可扩展的 LLM 个性化的方法，通过共享低秩基与紧凑的用户特异校正（外加秩一残差）分离适配参数，在降低每用户参数量与过拟合风险的同时提高泛化与推理效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of personalized survey response prediction using fine-tuned large language models (LLMs). This task poses unique challenges: limited per-user training data, scalability of model storage, and the need to exploit shared structure across survey questions. To address these issues, we propose Aplaud (Adaptive Personalized Low-rank and User-specific Nested Decomposition), a lightweight and scalable framework for LLM personalization. Aplaud extends the LoRA paradigm by separating adaptation into a frozen, shared low-rank basis and a compact user-specific correction, augmented with a rank-one residual for finer personalization. To further reduce per-user parameter cost and mitigate overfitting, the correction matrix can be factorized into an even lower-rank form. Empirical results demonstrate that Aplaud achieves efficient, scalable personalization across users while outperforming state-of-the-art LoRA-based personalized LLM approaches in both generalization and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [DCFA: Dual-view Causal-inspired Attribution for Failure Reasoning in LLM-based Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04749)
*Zehao Wang,Lanjun Wang,Shilong Jin,Junjie Chen,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DCFA是一个训练免疫的双模块故障归因框架，通过构建因果启发的依赖图并辅以局部反事实推理，能更准确地识别多智能体系统的决定性错误，实验显示显著提升归因准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统易产生推理与协调错误，现有归因方法常只暴露表层偏差或随着轨迹长度增加而出现上下文衰减，导致难以定位能逆转失败的真正初始错误。

Method: 提出了一个无训练框架DCFA，包括：1) 全局模块——将系统交互轨迹解析为结构化的因果启发依赖图以识别最早的决定性错误；2) 局部模块——对识别结果进行局部反事实式推理以细化归因并验证因果关系。

Result: 在Who&When基准上对六种LLM进行评估，DCFA在步骤级准确率上相比现有最优方法最高提升约8.27%。

Conclusion: DCFA通过结合全局的因果启发依赖图与局部的反事实启发推理，实现对多智能体系统故障追溯的精确归因，能够识别并修正“决定性错误”，在无需训练的前提下提升归因性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems have experienced rapid growth in recent years. Despite their promise, such systems remain fragile, frequently exhibiting reasoning and coordination errors that can lead to system-level failures. Failure attribution in such systems relies on tracing natural language interactions among agents to identify the decisive error, which refers to the earliest action whose correction can reverse system failure. There are two key challenges: 1) Shallow attribution: Existing methods often capture only minor deviations, such as incomplete retrievals or formatting errors, which verification mechanisms can correct, while missing the decisive cause of system failure. 2) Contextual degradation: As the length of the system traces increases, the model's reasoning ability rapidly deteriorates. To address these challenges, we propose DCFA, a training-free framework for failure attribution. DCFA integrates a global module that constructs structured causal-inspired dependency graphs from system traces to identify the initial decisive error, and a local module that applies local counterfactual-inspired reasoning to refine causal-inspired attribution. Experiments on the Who&When benchmark across six LLMs show that DCFA improves step-level accuracy by up to 8.27% over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [71] [Shadow Queries for Private Retrieval in Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04767)
*Xinguo Feng,Zhongkui Ma,Zihan Wang,Chuan Yan,Guowei Yang,Alsharif Abuadbba,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用生成的影子查询替代文档嵌入，通过语义分解和嵌入解耦有效降低嵌入反演风险且保留检索效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库中预计算的文档嵌入易被嵌入反演攻击还原文本，现有通过添加噪声或缩放嵌入的防御要么隐私不足要么显著损失检索效用，需寻找在隐私与效用间更优的折中方案。

Method: 利用生成式大语言模型为每个文档生成多样的“影子查询”（shadow queries），每个查询覆盖文档的不同语义侧面；将这些影子查询经编码器得到的向量存入向量数据库，替代文档的原始嵌入；检索时返回与影子查询相匹配的文档，从而通过语义分解与嵌入-文本的弱耦合抵抗嵌入反演攻击。

Result: 在多种IR数据集上，SHAQ将攻击的恢复率降至最低0.2104；相比基线可多防护最多19.50%的被恢复token；在检索效用上达到最高0.7967的MAP@10，并在若干设置下实现最多5.53%的效用提升，表明在隐私提升的同时能保持或改善检索性能。

Conclusion: SHAQ通过语义分解与嵌入解耦，在不直接修改原始文档嵌入的情况下，显著降低了嵌入反演攻击成功率，同时在多数评测上保持甚至略微提升检索效果，是对现有噪声注入类防御的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on information retrieval (IR) systems, such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), to incorporate domain-specific knowledge without costly re-training. These systems often store pre-computed document embeddings in cloud-based vector databases. However, such embeddings are vulnerable to embedding inversion attacks (EIAs), which can reconstruct their underlying text. Existing defenses, such as adding noise or scaling embeddings, often provide limited privacy or significantly reduce retrieval utility.
  We propose SHAQ (shadow query generation), a semantic-decomposition and embedding-decoupling defense against EIAs. SHAQ is based on the insight that EIAs rely on the strong coupling between an embedding and its original text. Instead of storing document embeddings directly, SHAQ uses a generative language model to create diverse shadow queries that capture different semantic aspects of each document. These queries are then encoded and stored in place of the original document embeddings, thereby decomposing document semantics and decoupling stored embeddings from the source text.
  Experiments across diverse IR datasets show that SHAQ substantially improves privacy while preserving retrieval utility, achieving a recovery rate as low as 0.2104, defending up to 19.50% more tokens than baseline defenses, and reaching up to 0.7967 MAP@10 with up to 5.53% utility improvement. These results demonstrate that semantic decomposition and embedding decoupling provide an effective alternative to directly modifying embeddings for defending against EIAs.

</details>


### [72] [Diffusion Language Models for Mobile Edge Agentic AI: Foundations, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04778)
*Chenqi Li,Minghui Min,Dusit Niyato,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型以并行迭代去噪为核心，天然支持部分完善与可控性，适合追求质量—延迟灵活性与鲁棒性的边缘智能代理，但在采样步数、离散化复杂性和能耗等方面仍面临挑战，需要在算法、压缩与系统协同方面进一步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对移动边缘代理的实时性、能耗、内存与通信限制，探索比自回归LLM更灵活的生成机制，以支持早退、部分重写和约束引导等能力，减少响应延迟与通信开销并提升在噪声或动态上下文下的鲁棒性。

Method: 本综述基于DLM的理论基础与近年工作，分析其在边缘部署下的体系结构、训练与推理加速、模型压缩、边缘/云协同、通信感知服务与物联网/无线应用，并梳理评估指标与实验基准。

Result: 文中系统归纳了DLM在资源受限环境的适配策略（如轻量化架构、蒸馏/量化、步数优化、分布式/分割推理方案），讨论了在IoT/无线场景的通信与隐私问题，并列出若干未解决问题：长上下文状态管理、分割推理协议、可信执行、多模态对齐与可复现基准。

Conclusion: 本文综述指出扩散语言模型（DLMs）通过并行、多次迭代的去噪生成，提供了与自回归Transformer不同的生成范式，能在移动边缘智能代理中实现可调的质量—延迟权衡、部分完善与约束修正，从而在带宽、延迟、隐私和鲁棒性受限场景具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a non-autoregressive alternative for mobile edge agentic artificial intelligence (AI) by refining tokens through iterative denoising rather than left-to-right decoding. Compared with autoregressive Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), DLMs can update multiple uncertain tokens in parallel and exploit bidirectional context throughout the generation process, enabling more flexible quality-latency trade-offs beyond fixed sequential decoding. These properties are particularly attractive for edge agents, where partial refinement, early exit, and constraint-guided correction can reduce response delay and communication overhead while improving robustness under noisy, incomplete, or dynamic contexts. This survey reviews DLM foundations and analyzes their suitability for edge settings under latency, memory, energy, bandwidth, privacy, and reliability constraints. We cover resource-efficient architectures, training and inference acceleration, compression, edge/cloud deployment, communication-aware serving, Internet of Things (IoT)/wireless applications, and evaluation of DLM-based agents. We further discuss open issues in long-context state management, split inference, trustworthy execution, multimodal grounding, and reproducible benchmarking. The goal is to connect DLM modeling properties, including bidirectionality, parallel refinement, controllability, and quality-latency elasticity, with system-level requirements of future mobile edge intelligence.

</details>


### [73] [DODR: Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04782)
*Weicai Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models formulate reasoning as token-level probabilistic sampling, which induces three fundamental defects in complex logical reasoning: error accumulation, probability substituting necessity, and the linear-chain information bottleneck. This paper proposes the Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space architecture (DODR), which reconstructs reasoning as reasoning-graph computation in a high-dimensional linear-algebraic space. Reasoning states are represented as snapshot vectors whose primitives are semantic units (phrases or sentences) rather than tokens, and each inference step is a deterministic matrix operation with no token sampling. Peirce's three inference types are formalized as three trainable matrix operators: a rank-deficient deduction operator (information collapse), a full-rank induction operator (information expansion), and an abduction operator defined as the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of deduction (information hypothesizing). We prove that the operator set is minimal and complete given Peirce's trichotomy, that no single "super-operator" can realize all three types (a rank obstruction), and that reasoning graphs are Turing-complete with contractive backflow converging by Banach's fixed-point theorem. Experiments on 503 sample records (420 deduplicated samples) across dedicated and end-to-end settings show: deduction loss converges to 1.40e-05; induction achieves 0.9996 generalization coverage with 20/20 hard vetoes on counterexamples; abduction solutions exceed the random baseline by 28x with judgment accuracies of 72.5% (58/80, Wilson 95% CI [61.9%, 81.1%]) and 81.7% (49/60, CI [70.1%, 89.4%]); frozen operators attain 100% (60/60) on unseen cross-domain deduction. The architecture provides a structural zero-hallucination guarantee and a three-layer continual-learning mechanism. All data and code are released.

</details>


### [74] [ProtLingo: Efficient Protein Language Modeling via Conditional Memory and Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04793)
*Mingrui Li,Sixian Shen,Minzhang Li,Ruiyi Zhang,Kexin Zhang,Jiakai Zhang,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtLingo通过局部可重用记忆与稀疏专家路由，使中等规模单序列PLM在突变敏感预测和结构相关任务上实现高效且竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩大量化的密集Transformer往往显著增加计算与参数成本，但对突变效应等任务的提升并不稳定；需要一种参数高效且保留长程结构信息的方法来提高突变敏感预测能力。

Method: 在预训练单序列骨干上并行添加两类模块：1) 条件局部记忆：将上下文残基表示映射为路由特异的离散码，将中心局部窗口组成潜在的N-gram地址，检索与重复局部序列上下文相关的可重用残差信号；2) 稀疏专家路由：将若干前馈块改造为Mixture-of-Experts层，包含共享与路由专家，实现残基依赖的计算而仅激活部分参数。

Result: 在蛋白质适应性预测、FLIP基准和有监督接触预测任务中，基于150M规模骨干的ProtLingo取得了具竞争力的结果：在突变效应预测上展现出较好的参数效率，并保留长程结构表征。

Conclusion: ProtLingo通过在预训练单序列Transformer骨干上增添条件局部记忆与稀疏专家路由机制，能在不简单扩大密集Transformer规模的情况下实现对突变敏感的高效表征，从而在蛋白质适应性预测与接触预测等任务上达到与更大或更昂贵模型可比的性能。

Abstract: Proteins perform diverse cellular functions, and even single amino-acid substitutions can alter stability, activity, or molecular interactions. Protein language models (PLMs) provide a scalable approach for modeling such sequence--function relationships from unlabeled sequences, but increasing the size of dense Transformer backbones often brings substantial computational cost without consistently improving mutation-sensitive prediction. We introduce ProtLingo, an efficient PLM framework that augments a pretrained single-sequence backbone with conditional local memory and sparse expert routing. ProtLingo maps contextual residue representations into route-specific discrete codes, composes centered local windows into latent $N$-gram addresses, and retrieves reusable residual signals associated with recurring local sequence contexts. In parallel, selected feed-forward blocks are upcycled into sparse Mixture-of-Experts layers with shared and routed experts, enabling residue-dependent computation while activating only a subset of parameters. Experiments on protein fitness prediction, FLIP benchmarks, and supervised contact prediction show that ProtLingo achieves competitive performance with a 150M-scale backbone, including strong parameter efficiency on mutation-effect prediction and preserved long-range structural representations.

</details>


### [75] [Whose record is this? Diagnosing and authorizing record use in personalized multimodal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04801)
*Xinyu Mao,Junsi Li,Chenyang Liu,Haoji Zhang,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出记录授权的P/E/S判定并定义视觉记忆误绑定，构建了RecordAuth-Diag诊断集揭示多模态模型中常见的未授权使用，展示类型化预生成授权与完整P∧E∧S验证可显著降低不安全释放，但以回忆率下降为代价，且支持判定是主要的改进来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：在上下文化视觉个性化场景中，系统可能检索到真实记录却错误地应用于错误的视觉主体，产生视觉记忆误绑定（VMM）。必须形式化何时允许记录影响回答，并设计诊断基准与缓解策略以度量和减少未授权使用。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 形式化定义记录授权为三要素：主体存在（P）、记录-边缘有效性（E）、答案支持（S）；将VMM定义为违反至少一项要素的情况；(2) 构建RecordAuth-Diag诊断集（3,690例），通过在保持查询、问题、记录文本与图像多重集不变的前提下仅改变一张图像–记录边缘来局部对比；(3) 采取卡片移除和随机重标记（nonce relabeling）来归因错误至所提供记录；(4) 在原始模型接口（Qwen、Phi、Gemma 家族）上测量未授权使用率与干净回忆率；(5) 评估缓解措施：类型化预生成授权（typed pre-generation authorization）和基于560例本地化DAVIS用例的完整P∧E∧S验证，并对不安全释放按support/edge/clean/boundary分类统计。

Result: 主要实验结果包括：在RecordAuth-Diag上原始模型存在显著未授权使用；例如Gemma-3-4B-IT达63.69%的局部未授权使用，伴随25.75%的干净回忆率；CoViP为26.02%，其Qwen骨干在相近干净回忆下为22.49%；类型化预生成授权将Qwen在RecordAuth-Diag上的卡片暴露从43.63%降至3.06%，但正向回忆率由86.26%降至60.90%；在560例DAVIS本地化用例上，top-1相关性释放与类型化授权释放相近（28.93% vs 28.39%），但不安全释放分别为6.79%与0.89%；在移除的33个额外不安全用例中，按原因分布为：27个support、4个edge、2个clean、0个boundary，表明改进主要来自对支持(S)的判定而非仅边缘(E)检查。作者还指出外观提供E的证据仅在P成立时有效，认证主体令牌能填补缺失的存在证据。声明的结论限于所评估的契约（contracts），不涉及自然普遍率、同意或视觉身份等伦理层面。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：需要以形式化“记录授权”（record authorization）条件来判断视觉记忆是否被错误绑定（VMM）；在受控诊断基准上，多款多模态接口出现显著的本地未授权使用（unauthorized use）；类型化的预生成授权和验证完整的P∧E∧S检查可显著降低不安全释放，但代价是回忆率下降；支持（S）判定比边界（E）判定对不安全释放的减少作用更大；外观仅在主体存在（P）已满足时才为E提供证据，验证主体存在的认证令牌能作为充分性控制。

Abstract: Contextualized visual personalization can retrieve a true record yet apply it to the wrong visual subject. We formalize when a record may condition an answer as \emph{record authorization}: subject presence ($P$), record-edge validity ($E$), and answer support ($S$) must all hold. We call violations visual memory misbinding (VMM). We construct RecordAuth-Diag, a 3,690-case matched diagnostic suite that changes one image--record edge while holding the query, question, record text, and image multiset fixed. Card removal and nonce relabeling attribute these failures to supplied records. Raw-bank failures span Qwen-, Phi-, and Gemma-family interfaces: Gemma-3-4B-IT reaches 63.69\% local unauthorized use at 25.75\% clean recall. CoViP remains at 26.02\%, versus 22.49\% for its Qwen backbone at similar clean recall. Typed pre-generation authorization reduces Qwen card exposure on RecordAuth-Diag from 43.63\% to 3.06\%, while positive recall changes from 86.26\% to 60.90\%. Full $P\wedge E\wedge S$ validation uses 560 localized DAVIS cases: top-1 relevance and typed authorization have comparable release (28.93\% and 28.39\%) but 6.79\% and 0.89\% unsafe release, respectively. Of the 33 additional unsafe cases removed, 27 are support, 4 edge, 2 clean, and 0 boundary cases. Thus the observed increment is an $E\wedge S$ decision dominated by support, not an edge check alone. Appearance supplies $E$ evidence only conditional on $P$; authenticated subject tokens instantiate the missing presence witness as a sufficiency control. The claims concern the evaluated contracts, not natural prevalence, consent, or visual identity

</details>


### [76] [Hierarchical Possession-Aware Graph Pointer Network for Pass Receiver Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04803)
*Jingyi Wang,Da Li,Kaixin Wang,Zhangqin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HPGPN通过图结构建模当前交互、融合事件上下文并引入控球级时间动态，以指针式评分机制在匿名、部分可见的广播式场景中改进了传球接收者预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广播式freeze-frame事件数据中，球员可见性和身份均不稳定，缺乏完整轨迹与一致ID，因而需要一种能在匿名可见候选、对手压迫和部分观察的条件下结合当前交互、事件上下文与控球历史进行接收者选择的模型。

Method: 提出HPGPN模型：使用图网络表示当前传球情境、固定事件上下文向量和基于控球历史的双分支时间动态模块，逐层融合空间、上下文和历史证据，并用glimpse pointer head对可见队友候选对象进行评分，输出可变长度候选预测。

Result: 在公开足球事件与freeze-frame数据集上的实验表明，HPGPN在传球接收者选择任务上优于比较基线；消融实验证明图交互建模、固定事件上下文和双分支控球历史模块均对性能有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 在广播风格、事件中心的足球数据设置下，分层融合空间、上下文与历史信息的图指针网络能有效提高传球接收者选择性能；模型组件的设计（图建模、事件上下文、双分支历史）分别带来性能提升。

Abstract: Pass receiver selection is a fundamental task in football analytics, aiming to predict the intended receiver under a given game state. This task is challenging with event-centered freeze-frame observations, a broadcast-like setting that provides only partial and variable player visibility without complete trajectories or stable player identities. The model must therefore reason over anonymous visible candidates, opponent pressure, and recent context under partial observation. To address this setting, we propose a Hierarchical Possession-aware Graph Pointer Network (HPGPN), which formulates pass receiver selection as variable-size candidate prediction over visible teammates. HPGPN jointly models current player interactions, local event context, and possession-level temporal dynamics. It represents the current pass situation with a graph, incorporates fixed event context, and uses dynamic possession history to capture how the attacking sequence evolves. Candidate representations are refined hierarchically by integrating spatial, contextual, and historical evidence, and a glimpse pointer head scores the receiver candidates. Experiments on public football event and freeze-frame data show that HPGPN improves pass receiver selection performance. Ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of graph-based interaction modeling, fixed event context, and dual-branch dynamic possession-history modeling.

</details>


### [77] [MedFlow: Class-Aware Multi-Scale Generation for Medical Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04804)
*Yanhao Huang,Shibo Feng,Wanjin Feng,Peilin Zhao,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedFlow：一个类感知多尺度流匹配生成框架，通过VQ多尺度表示与Token Marginal Guidance强化稀有类时序特征，提高合成样本的下游效用和采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成时序方法侧重整体分布与时间动力学的匹配，但临床信息往往在多尺度上出现且少数类特征易被主流模式遮蔽，导致合成数据对下游不平衡任务的效用受限。

Method: 引入向量量化（VQ）多尺度分词器以在互补的时间分辨率上表示序列；采用基于流（flow）匹配的生成框架进行样本生成；在流匹配训练目标中加入Token Marginal Guidance，将类条件的token统计信息注入，以引导生成器朝向类特定token区域，强化少数类特征同时保留总体和尾部分布。

Result: 在四个公共数据集（涵盖电子病历、EEG、ECG）上，MedFlow在下游预测任务上优于近期扩散基线：平均AUPRC提高5.8%、Context-FID降低88.6%、采样吞吐量提升3.8倍。

Conclusion: MedFlow通过类感知的多尺度流匹配结合向量量化多尺度分词器和Token Marginal Guidance，有效增强少数类时序模式，同时保持全局与尾部分布，在EHR/EEG/ECG数据上提升下游预测性能（平均AUPRC+5.8%）、显著降低Context-FID（-88.6%）并加快采样速度（3.8×）。

Abstract: Synthetic medical time-series generation can alleviate data scarcity and support the development of reliable clinical prediction models. However, existing methods mainly focus on matching the overall distribution and temporal dynamics of real data, which does not necessarily ensure strong downstream utility on imbalanced medical datasets. Clinically informative patterns often occur at heterogeneous temporal scales, while rare minority-class characteristics can be obscured by dominant population patterns. To address these challenges, we propose MedFlow, a class-aware multi-scale flow matching framework for medical time-series synthesis. MedFlow employs a vector-quantized multi-scale tokenizer to represent medical sequences at complementary temporal resolutions, capturing both coarse clinical trends and fine-grained dynamics. We further introduce Token Marginal Guidance, which incorporates class-conditional token statistics directly into the flow matching process to steer generation toward class-specific regions of the learned tokens. This mechanism strengthens minority-class patterns, while preserving the global and tail distributions of real data. Experiments on four public datasets covering electronic health records, EEG, and ECG signals demonstrate that MedFlow consistently outperforms recent state-of-the-art diffusion-based baselines across downstream prediction tasks. On average, it improves AUPRC by 5.8%, reduces Context-FID by 88.6%, and achieves 3.8$\times$ higher sampling throughput.

</details>


### [78] [When Financial Fine-tuning Fails: A Three-Level Detectability Analysis of Numerical Hallucination in Domain-Adapted Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04806)
*Xiaodong Li,Peiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者在受控微调实验中发现：领域微调削弱了模型对数值的克制，数值增强措施未能纠正且常使幻觉更严重，呼吁按多层可检测性评估幻觉并在部署时引入显式落地/回避机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证先前将数值幻觉归因于数值推理不足的假设，评估领域自适应与数值监督在金融报告摘要生成中对数值幻觉的实际影响。

Method: 在成本可控的条件下对比三类模型：基础指令调优模型（Base）、领域语言适配模型（FT-A）和带数值增强的领域模型（FT-A+B+C）；提出三层可检测性分类（显性货币伪造、隐性显式行业约定数字、隐性隐含定量断言），并对不同模型在这些层次上的幻觉率进行系统评估。

Result: Base模型幻觉率近零（5.4%），FT-A在显性层面达到82.5%，FT-A+B+C升至98%；数值监管并未抑制幻觉，反而放大；定位主要机制为“模板注入”——模型插入记忆化的典型数值而非基于输入推断。

Conclusion: 数值幻觉并非主要由数值推理能力不足驱动，而是由领域微调过程中数值约束被削弱所致；所谓的数值增强监督反而通过“模板注入”放大了幻觉现象。

Abstract: Financial large language models are increasingly deployed for summarization of reports and disclosures, where numerical hallucination poses significant practical risks. While prior work often attributes such hallucination to insufficient numerical reasoning, this assumption has not been systematically tested under controlled fine-tuning settings. In this paper, we conduct a cost-effective, controlled study of numerical hallucination in financial summarization across three model variants: a base instruction-tuned model, a domain language-adapted model (FT-A), and a numeracy-enhanced domain model (FT-A+B+C). We introduce a three-level detectability taxonomy distinguishing between overt hallucination (currency-denominated fabrication), covert-explicit hallucination (professional-convention numbers), and covert-implicit hallucination (ungrounded quantitative claims). Our results reveal that domain fine-tuning substantially degrades numerical restraint at all detectability levels. While the Base model maintains near-zero hallucination rates (5.4\%), FT-A exhibits 82.5\% overt hallucination and FT-A+B+C reaches 98\%. Contrary to intuition, numeracy supervision amplifies rather than mitigates hallucination across all levels. We identify template injection---the insertion of memorized canonical values regardless of input content---as a primary hallucination mechanism in fine-tuned models. These findings demonstrate that numerical hallucination in financial summarization is driven by the degradation of numerical restraint through domain adaptation, not by insufficient numerical reasoning. We recommend that evaluation protocols assess hallucination across all detectability levels and that deployment practices include explicit mechanisms for grounding-aware generation or abstention.

</details>


### [79] [CPR-IE:A Compression-Prediction-Resource Intelligence Efficiency Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04809)
*Xiantao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CPR-IE（Compression–Prediction–Resource Intelligence Efficiency）作为在部署约束下比较智能系统的协议相关排序，基于表示经济性、预测质量和资源负担，导出 I(C,P,T) 形式并证明一系列良好性质与统计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中仅靠预测精度不足以比较系统，应把模型尺寸/压缩和运算能耗等资源负担纳入统一、可比较且具有良好数理性质的效率排序框架；受最小描述长度、算法复杂性、公正评分规则、变分推断和朗道尔原则等启发，但目标是构造一个满足自然公设的表示形式。

Method: 从两个基本问题入手（资源如何被表示；属性如何聚合），采用比例增量组成和上下文独立的比率响应公设，得到对压缩、预测和负担的幂律响应与对数累积负担形式；通过解析证明（Pareto、单位不变、边界、可交换性等）并引入 translog 父模型表征交互；接着给出可识别性、次高斯有限样本排序界、在指数不确定性下的稳健选择与确定性遗憾界等统计/算法结果。

Result: 得到 I(C,P,T)（经参考归一化）的具体效率表示，证明若干公理化和统计性质（如帕累托一致性、单位不变性、贸易关系、排名稳定区间、跨任务聚合、交互建模、有限样本置信保证、稳健选择与遗憾上界），并分析了度量选择的动机与局限。

Conclusion: CPR-IE 提供了一个可操作且公理化的效率表示，用以在资源受限情境下对系统进行稳定、可比较的排序；它是一个构造性的度量表示（非普适智能定义），并带有一致性、标度不变性、边界行为、交互建模和有限样本/决定性选择保证。

Abstract: Comparing intelligent systems under deployment constraints requires more than predictiveaccuracy.This paper develops Compression-Prediction-Resource Intelligence Efficiency (CPR-IE) as a protocol-relative ordering by representational economy, predictive quality, and resourceburden. The analysis separates two questions-how raw resource consumption is represented, andhow the resulting attributes are aggregated. Proportional-increment composition uniquely yieldslogarithmic cumulative burden, and context-independent ratio response yields power responsesto compression, prediction, and burden; with reference normalization the representation is I(C,P,T).We prove Pareto consistency, unit invariance, boundary behavior, trade-off identities, ranking-stability regions, and cross-task aggregation. A translog parent model makes interaction restrictions explicit, and further results establish cardinal and ordinal identification, sub-Gaussianfinite-sample ranking guarantees, robust selection under exponent uncertainty, and deterministicregret bounds. Minimum description length, algorithmic complexity, proper scoring rules, varia-tional inference, and Landauer's principle motivate measurement choices but do not entail theformula. CPR-IE is a constructed efficiency representation, not a universal law or a definition ofintelligence itself.

</details>


### [80] [Long Horizon Transformer Quantile Fault Prediction for Multi Site Industrial Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04840)
*David J Poland,Daniele Ravi,Na Helian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TQRNN30d：用双阶段QRNN把每小时81通道映射为324维分位态，720小时（30天）为文档输入，多流时序融合分类器融合量化态、动态协变量、静态元数据和168小时潜在历史，辅以不稳定感知信号，实现在同类九工厂的留机长期故障预测上优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期预测维护需在以天为单位的规划窗口上区分缓慢演进的退化与正常运行内的波动，提前、稳健地触发维护决策以降低停机与成本；提出的条件分位表示旨在捕捉通道分布性与不确定性以改善长期判别。

Method: 双阶段量化回归神经网络（QRNN）作为特征提取器：每小时81通道→324维分位态表示；将720个有序小时片段构成30天文档输入给多流时序融合分类器。分类器融合量化状态、动态协变量、通道级静态元数据与168小时潜在历史流，采用门控残差、因果递归编码和元数据条件的跨模态注意力；引入基于持续一步预测误差发散的有界不稳定感知信号作为长时记忆调制辅助。

Result: TQRNN30d在30天预测上取得：F1=79.97%、召回=80.18%、精确=81.82%、准确率=82.39%、ROC‑AUC=0.820；在7/14/30天的固定阈值对比中领先于18个基线方法，14天时F1优势最大。评估采用机器不重叠的43/14/15划分，覆盖72台设备、9个工厂。

Conclusion: 在受控的同质化设备群内，条件分位表示+多流融合能在30天级别区分缓慢退化与正常波动，提升长期预维护分类性能；但方法的站点间/设备间泛化性和外部可复现性未被证明。

Abstract: Long-horizon predictive maintenance requires models to distinguish slowly evolving degradation from normal operating-regime variation over planning windows measured in days rather than hours. This paper evaluates whether an explicit conditional-quantile representation provides an informative classifier interface for this problem. The proposed TQRNN30d framework combines a dual-stage quantile regression neural network (QRNN) feature extractor with a multi-stream temporal fusion classifier. Each hourly word of 81-channel machine behaviour is mapped to a 324-dimensional quantile-state representation, and 720 ordered hourly words form the 30-day document supplied to the long-horizon model. The classifier fuses quantile states with dynamic covariates, channel-level static metadata, and a 168-hour latent-history stream using gated residual processing, causal recurrent encoding, and metadata-conditioned cross-modal attention. A bounded instability-aware signal derived from sustained one-word-ahead prediction-error divergence provides auxiliary memory modulation at the longest horizon. Evaluation uses a machine-disjoint 43/14/15 train/validation/test allocation across 72 machines in nine manufacturing facilities. At 30 days, TQRNN30d achieves 79.97% F1, 80.18% recall, 81.82% precision, 82.39% accuracy, and 0.820 ROC-AUC. It leads all 18 evaluated baselines at the 7-, 14-, and 30-day fixed-threshold comparisons, with the largest F1 advantage at 14 days. The results support held-out-machine performance within the observed homogeneous nine-facility fleet, but do not establish unseen-site, cross-equipment, or cross-sector generalisation.

</details>


### [81] [ElderBench: Benchmarking Autonomous Mobile Agents for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04850)
*Weide Zhan,Qumu Shaqu,Yuanqing Liu,Peng Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Kam Him Lam,Ning Gu,Zhan Hu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ElderBench为老年用户手机操作提供真实语言样本并展示：老年人的自然指令在结构与含义上与现有基准有显著偏差，现有GUI代理在此类指令上性能显著下降，需设计更自适应与可解释的年龄包容型代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向移动GUI的基准多以显式、目标导向的指令为主，不能反映老年用户在真实交互中常见的间接表达、指称歧义与欠明确请求；这种不匹配会阻碍移动GUI代理在老年群体中的可靠部署。

Method: 从老年受试者处自然诱导并收集249条手机操作任务（20个应用），从句法、语义、语用三层面对比分析老年指令与现有基准；在在线/离线设置下评估主流GUI代理与视觉-语言模型；通过控制性指令规范化实验、失败案例分析与细粒度语言特征统计来归因性能下降的语言因素。

Result: 提出ElderBench：首个面向老年场景的移动GUI评测基准，包含从老年人自然引导下收集的249个手机任务（覆盖20个应用）；并系统表征了老年指令与现有基准在句法、语义与语用上的差异，实验证明主流GUI代理与视觉-语言模型在处理老年导向指令时性能明显下降；通过指令规范化、失败分析与细粒度语言特征分析，定位了老年语言模式导致失败的关键因素。

Conclusion: 为适配老年用户，需在数据收集、模型训练与交互策略上考虑老年特有的语言特征（如间接表达与歧义），并增强代理的澄清能力、个性化策略与可解释性，以实现更可靠的部署。

Abstract: While autonomous mobile agents hold great potential for assisting older adults with smartphone usage, existing GUI benchmarks mainly rely on explicit, goal-oriented instructions and rarely capture the naturally occurring language patterns of older users, such as indirect speech, referential ambiguity, and under-specified requests. This mismatch between benchmark instructions and real-world elderly interactions may hinder reliable agent deployment. To address this gap, we present ElderBench, the first benchmark for evaluating mobile GUI agents in authentic elderly-oriented scenarios. ElderBench is constructed from 249 naturally elicited smartphone tasks collected from older adults across 20 applications. We first characterize the linguistic divergence between elderly instructions and existing GUI benchmark instructions from syntactic, semantic, and pragmatic perspectives. We then evaluate mainstream GUI agents and Vision-Language Models under both online and offline settings, revealing substantial performance degradation when handling elderly-oriented instructions. Through controlled instruction normalization, failure analysis, and fine-grained linguistic feature analysis, we further identify how elderly-specific language patterns contribute to agent failures. Our findings provide actionable design insights toward more adaptive, interpretable, and age-inclusive GUI agents for older adults.

</details>


### [82] [MM-IFEval-Pro: A Multilingual and Attack-Resistant Benchmark for Instruction-Following in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04859)
*Changming Xiao,Zhenliang Ni,Jinhui He,Han Shu,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个覆盖中英、包含丰富指令劫持场景的多模态指令遵循评测MM-IFEval-Pro，并通过含中文与对抗指令的RL训练数据提升模型表现与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态指令遵循基准在语言覆盖（尤其中文）和对抗/安全场景上不足，无法充分评估现实世界中的多语言与安全敏感问题，需要一个更全面、具有对抗性测试的评测集合并探索相应训练策略。

Method: 构建包含4大任务类、24子类、8类指令与52子类指令的多模态指令遵循基准（每样本平均3.0项约束）；同时构造并使用一套富含中文与对抗指令的强化学习训练集（RL），对模型进行训练与评估以检验横向任务与跨语言泛化能力。

Result: 引入的MM-IFEval-Pro与对抗性中文RL数据能显著提升模型在评测上的性能，并有效迁移到其他多模态基准，显示出较强的跨任务与跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: MM-IFEval-Pro弥补了多模态指令遵循评测在语言覆盖与敌意安全场景上的空白，配合包含中文与对抗性指令的强化学习训练集，能显著提升模型在该评测上的表现并在其他主流多模态基准上实现有效迁移。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) rapidly advance in image understanding, cross-modal reasoning, and complex instruction execution, instruction-following capability has become a key indicator of their reliability and practicality. However, existing multimodal instruction-following benchmarks still suffer from limited language coverage and insufficient adversarial safety scenarios, making them inadequate for evaluating real-world multilingual and safety-sensitive settings. To address these gaps, we present MM-IFEval-Pro, a multimodal instruction-following benchmark covering Chinese and English tasks as well as diverse instruction hijacking cases. MM-IFEval-Pro includes 4 major task categories and 24 subcategories and 8 instruction categories with 52 subcategories, with each sample containing an average of 3.0 constraints to realistically simulate complex instruction scenarios. We further construct a reinforcement-learning training set enriched with Chinese and adversarial instructions, which significantly improves model performance on MM-IFEval-Pro and transfers effectively to other mainstream multimodal benchmarks, demonstrating strong cross-task and cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [83] [MZ-Rain: Moisture-Budget-Guided Zero-Inflated Model for Station-Level Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04864)
*Yifang Zhang,Shengwu Xiong,Henan Wang,Wenjie Yin,Yuqiang Zhang,Chen Zhou,Hua Chen,Qile Zhao,Pengfei Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将水汽收支物理先验融入多分支sLSTM并结合自适应Tweedie与湿干辅助任务，解决零膨胀与物理一致性问题，从而改善站点级降水短时预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列方法未能区分不同气象变量在降水形成中的不同角色，且站点降水数据呈严重零膨胀，影响模型对降水量与发生的联合估计。

Method: 基于水汽收支方程构建物理驱动的多分支sLSTM框架（每个分支对应一个过程路径），输出端采用自适应Tweedie模型对降水量进行建模，并辅以降水发生（干湿判别）作为辅助任务联合训练。

Result: 在多地域、多气候条件下的实验中，MZ-Rain在CSI、FAR、MSE、MAE等指标上均优于强基线，对大强度降水事件的捕捉能力明显提升。

Conclusion: MZ-Rain通过将降水形成过程分解为由水汽储量、输送、蒸发与持续性等物理过程驱动的分支，并结合自适应Tweedie分布与湿干判别辅助任务，显著提升了站点级降水逐时预报的定性与定量性能，尤其对强降水事件具备更好的预报能力。

Abstract: Accurate station-level precipitation nowcasting is critical for agriculture, water resource management, and disaster prevention, which typically is formulated as a time series forecasting problem. However, conventional time-series modeling techniques face two major challenges in addressing station-level precipitation nowcasting: (1) Lack of Physics-Guided Modeling}, where meteorological variables are treated as a homogeneous set without accounting for their distinct roles in precipitation formation, leads to predictions that deviate from the physical processes governing precipitation. (2) Severe zero inflation in precipitation, where dry intervals dominate the dataset, obscuring meaningful precipitation patterns and complicating the predictive modeling. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{MZ-Rain}, a moisture-budget-guided zero-inflated sLSTM framework for station-level precipitation nowcasting. Guided by the moisture budget equation, MZ-Rain decomposes the precipitation formation process into process-specific pathways corresponding to moisture storage, moisture transport, surface evaporation, and precipitation persistence, and captures their temporal evolution through dedicated sLSTM branches. To account for the zero-inflated nature of precipitation, MZ-Rain introduces an adaptive Tweedie modeling strategy that adaptively modulates the rainfall mean while jointly learning precipitation occurrence as an auxiliary task, enabling the model to better balance dry-wet discrimination and quantitative precipitation estimation. Extensive experiments across diverse geographical and climatic regimes demonstrate that MZ-Rain consistently outperforms strong baselines on multiple evaluation metrics, including CSI, FAR, MSE, and MAE. In particular, the model exhibits superior skill in forecasting heavy precipitation events, while benefiting from physically grounded process modeling.

</details>


### [84] [CoSkill: Joint Reinforcement Learning of Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents for Hierarchical Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04865)
*Jinyuan Feng,Dongmin Li,Yiqun Chen,Yang Gao,Xing Chen,Huimu Wang,Zhiqiang Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoSkill：将固定的元技能流程拟人为可学习的Meta-Skill Agent并与推理Agent共享骨干端到端联合训练，从而实现技能与推理的协同进化，显著提升样本效率与最终性能（ALFWorld 98.4%，WebShop 90.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库方法要么将技能演化与策略优化断开，要么把元技能实现为固定工作流，导致技能无法灵活进化也无法与推理策略共同适配；需要一个能联合学习元技能与推理策略、支持技能动态演化的框架。

Method: 构建两层代理：Reasoning Agent负责在任务层调度并基于检索到的任务技能与其子技能序列作出动作；Meta-Skill Agent作为可学习的元技能工作流，接受任务绩效反馈并更新step技能。两者共享单一骨干网络，采用多智能体协作强化学习框架对分层技能库进行联合训练。实验在ALFWorld与WebShop上进行对比，包含样本效率与壁钟时间评估。

Result: CoSkill jointly trains a learnable Meta-Skill Agent and a Reasoning Agent with a shared backbone over a hierarchical skill library, enabling end-to-end co-adaptation. On ALFWorld and WebShop it achieves 98.4% and 90.6% success (+3.5 and +6.2 percentage points over baselines) with superior early-stage sample efficiency, asymptotic performance, and wall-clock efficiency.

Conclusion: 通过将元技能建模为可训练的代理并与推理代理共享参数，CoSkill实现了任务表现驱动的技能细化与策略-技能的协同适配，解决了传统技能库中技能被动管理或固定流程的局限性，从而在若干交互式环境上带来稳定且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Skill libraries improve the sample efficiency of agentic reinforcement learning (RL) by enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedural knowledge. Yet existing paradigms exhibit structural shortcomings: they either decouple skill evolution from policy optimization or instantiate meta-skills as fixed workflows. Both treat skills as passive objects to be managed, limiting the flexible evolution of skills and their co-adaptation with the reasoning agent. To address the limitations, we propose CoSkill, a unified multi-agent RL framework that recasts the static meta-skill workflow as a learnable Meta-Skill Agent and jointly trains it with a Reasoning Agent over a hierarchical skill library. By modeling the Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents as a cooperative team sharing a single backbone, CoSkill enables end-to-end co-adaptation: the Reasoning Agent conditions its actions on a retrieved task skill and step skills selected from its child set, while its task performance guides the Meta-Skill Agent in refining those step skills. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that CoSkill substantially outperforms prior skill-based and RL baselines, achieving success rates of 98.4% and 90.6%, respectively (+3.5 and +6.2 pp). As shown in Figure 1, CoSkill achieves superior early-stage sample efficiency, asymptotic performance, and wall-clock efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/jinyuan-cookie/CoSkill.

</details>


### [85] [LLM-Assisted Behavioural and Scenario Augmentation for Agent-Based Energy Adoption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04866)
*Iias Faiud,Hossein Khaleghy,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将受限（可解释）的 LLM 生成行为规范与结构化情景规范融入已校准的能源 ABM，可在可控且可复现的前提下提高采纳预测的灵活性与政策相关性，且不会引入不合理的系统动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用 LLM 替代采纳模型会带来可解释性、可复现性和行为有效性问题。本工作动机是利用 LLM 强化情景设计与行为假设，同时保持模型的技术经济基础和可验证性，确保政策分析的可靠性。

Method: 提出一个混合框架：在已校准的爱尔兰奶牛农场光伏（PV）采纳 ABM 中，保留原有技术经济采纳机制，加入由 LLM 生成并受限的行为风格（保守、平衡、乐观）和规则验证的情景规范；通过多策略政策设置、蒙特卡洛世界与随机种子进行实验，比较行为修正前后的采纳结果。

Result: 在多种政策与随机设置下，模型呈现稳定且经济上合理的行为：行为修正保持采纳结果有界且随行为姿态单调变化，最多可实现约 13% 的采纳提升；未观察到不稳定或不现实的饱和动态。

Conclusion: 在受限行为规则和结构化情景规范下，LLM 辅助的规范设计可以在保持原有技术经济采纳机制的同时，为代理基础模型（ABM）提供可控、可复现且政策相关的行为修饰，且结果表现为有界、单调并在经济上合理。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) create opportunities to enrich simulation-based energy policy analysis, particularly by supporting structured behavioural assumptions and exploratory techno-economic scenarios. However, directly replacing adoption models with LLM reasoning raises concerns regarding interpretability, reproducibility, and behavioural validity. This paper proposes a hybrid framework for LLM-assisted specification design, integrating bounded behavioural rubrics and structured scenario specifications into a calibrated agent-based model (ABM) of solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption by Irish dairy farms. The proposed approach preserves the original techno-economic adoption mechanism while augmenting it with bounded behavioural modulation and scenario-driven uncertainty analysis. Behavioural effects are represented through interpretable conservative, balanced, and optimistic rubrics, while future policy and market conditions are explored through fixed, rule-validated scenario specifications. Experimental results across multiple policy settings, Monte Carlo worlds, and random seeds demonstrate stable and economically plausible behaviour, with adoption outcomes remaining bounded and monotonic across behavioural regimes. The framework achieves up to approximately 13% behavioural adoption increase relative to the corresponding logistic case without producing unstable or unrealistic saturation dynamics. The results demonstrate that LLM-assisted specifications can be integrated into calibrated energy ABMs in a controlled, reproducible, and policy-relevant manner.

</details>


### [86] [From Interaction Traces to Persistent Skills: Online Evolution for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04869)
*Longtao Hu,Xiao Liang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并评估了一个在线技能演化框架：把交互轨迹和评估反馈转成有版本的可复用过程库，迭代时使用冻结快照执行，更新在下一迭代可见。与空库对照实验表明，演化库在四个领域中均能提高评估得分（提高幅度5.7–18.6个百分点），但效果随领域而异且存在修订难以回溯的现象（GIMP中的修订 churn）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补计算机代理在GUI中获得的程序性知识仅为一次性、不被保留的问题，评估把采集到的交互和反馈持续组织成可复用技能库对长期表现的增益及其行为动态。

Method: 将交互轨迹与评估者反馈编码为持久、版本化的技能条目；每次迭代在冻结的库快照上运行，基于证据的技能更新加入到下一迭代的快照中。与配置匹配的空库对照，在相同动作生成与GUI映射堆栈、任务集、迭代周期下比较性能。并在GIMP上做可追溯性分析以观察跨任务检索与修订行为。

Result: Full（演化库）在四个OSWorld领域的后暖身阶段均显著高于空库（均值差5.7–18.6个百分点），但增益的时间稳定性依领域而异；GIMP实验显示技能会跨任务被检索并发生修订 churn，接受的重复编辑仍可能丢失原始任务来源。

Conclusion: 可版本化、可审计的技能库能作为共享的程序化记忆，提升固定的计算机操作栈性能，但其收益有条件性，且多次修订不一定能恢复技能的原始语境与来源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can execute increasingly complex tasks in graphical interfaces, but their interaction experience is typically transient: procedural knowledge acquired from one rollout is not systematically retained, refined, and reused in later tasks. Existing skill libraries provide external procedural knowledge, yet their incremental value over the same agent operating without skills, as well as their longitudinal dynamics under repeated interaction, remain insufficiently characterized. We present an online skill-evolution framework that converts interaction trajectories and evaluator feedback into a persistent, versioned library of reusable procedures. Each iteration executes against a frozen library snapshot, and evidence-guided skill updates become available in subsequent iterations without changing model parameters. We compare the full evolving-library system with a configuration-matched empty-library control across four OSWorld application domains under the same fixed action-generation and GUI-grounding stack, task sets, and iteration horizons. Following a five-iteration empty-library warm-up, Full attains a higher post-warm-up mean evaluator score in all four observed domain runs, with mean differences ranging from 5.7 to 18.6 percentage points and domain-dependent temporal stability. In GIMP, provenance-aware analysis reveals retrieval across task-of-origin boundaries and revision churn, where repeated accepted edits fail to recover the originating task. These findings characterize evolving skill libraries as auditable, shared procedural memory that can improve a fixed computer-use stack, while showing that their benefits are conditional and repeated revision does not guarantee recovery. Code is released at https://github.com/LongtaoHu/Skill-Evo4GUI.

</details>


### [87] [CHAMP: Cross-domain Hybrid Architecture for Matchmaking and Prediction in Online Multi-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04870)
*Kai Wang,Ge Fan,Chaoyun Zhang,Yuyang Jiang,Yuze Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CHAMP，利用跨模式(player-mode)特征和单一共享的领域感知网络DAWN，解决MOBA匹配中冷启动、分布不一致与数据稀疏问题，实现更平衡的队伍重分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对CUPID在真实部署中遇到的三大瓶颈——冷启动（缺少本模式历史）、不同段位间技能分布差异、极端技能段数据稀缺——提出解决方案以提升跨玩家群体的匹配质量。

Method: 构建混合域特征：短期跨模式时间序列（切片标注目标域特征）+每模式的长期/实时/队伍统计；设计DAWN，包括域感知知识提取器(DAKE)和面向时序/空间/置换的不变编码器(DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE)，在单一网络内联合学习模式条件化表示与去偏置；在线用一套模型服务多模式，配合每模式的位置满意阈值。

Result: 离线：67.73%预测准确率，优于注意力和序列基线；在线：跨整个联赛段位的A/B测试显示不平衡比赛下降，低段位5分钟击杀碾压率下降上限20.73%（并指出每模式仅需位置满意阈值作为模式相关配置）。

Conclusion: CHAMP 在离线预测上以67.73%胜率预测准确率优于各类注意力与序列基线；在线A/B测试显示整个段位范围内不平衡比赛显著下降，低段位5分钟击杀碾压率最多降低20.73%。

Abstract: Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) games rely on matchmaking to maintain competitive balance. Our prior work, CUPID, framed matchmaking as an assignment re-optimization problem and showed that a single-mode win-rate predictor can meaningfully rebalance teams. However, deploying such a system across diverse player populations exposes three practical bottlenecks: most queueing players lack sufficient in-mode match history (cold start), skill distributions shift drastically across rank tiers (distribution inconsistency), and extreme skill segments are severely data-starved.
  We present CHAMP, a cross-domain matchmaking framework that resolves these deployment bottlenecks. To address data sparsity and cold starts, CHAMP replaces the target-mode-only player profile with a hybrid domain feature collection: a timestamp-ordered cross-mode short-term sequence whose slices are annotated with target-domain features, plus per-mode breakdowns of long-term, real-time and team statistics. We further propose the Domain-Aware Win-rate Network (DAWN): a Domain-aware Knowledge Extractor (DAKE) compiles target-mode attributes into learnable representations that feed Domain-Aware Temporal/Spatial/Permutation OmniNet Encoders (DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE), so that mode-conditioned representations and per-mode debiasing are learned jointly inside a single shared network. Online, one trained DAWN serves every supported mode, with per-mode position-satisfaction thresholds as the only mode-specific knob.
  Offline, DAWN achieves 67.73% win-rate prediction accuracy, outperforming all evaluated attention and sequence baselines. Online A/B tests across the entire League ladder of a large-scale MOBA game, from novice players up to the top-expert players served by Elite Mode, demonstrate consistent drops in imbalanced matches. For lower-tier players, CHAMP reduces the 5-minute kill crushing rate by up to 20.73%.

</details>


### [88] [AutoLR: Automating the Path from Research to Launch Review in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04871)
*Qi Zhang,Yanlin Chen,Wenchao Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoLR is an autonomous harness for moving recommender-system ideas from research to full-traffic launch by combining LLM agents with deterministic controllers, employing a multi-expert council, evidence-weighted selector, and layered knowledge to manage long-running experiments on NetEase’s DASHEN.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Industrial recommender improvement is an iterative, multi-day research-and-engineering process involving literature review, reproduction/adaptation, implementation, offline evaluation, and online A/B testing, culminating in a Launch Review. Existing LLM aids help local tasks but lack a coordinating harness to reliably automate and orchestrate across the end-to-end pipeline and long-running experimental cycles.

Method: AutoLR is a research-to-launch harness that combines LLM agents for semantic reasoning and code generation with deterministic controllers for execution and state management. It implements three core mechanisms: (1) a multi-expert council that debates and adversarially reviews candidate proposals; (2) a deterministic evidence-weighted exploration–exploitation selector that allocates limited trial budget across candidates and uses Council reranking; and (3) a layered knowledge system that fuses external research, production-system knowledge, domain-specific information (DASHEN), and posterior experimental evidence. The system automates portions of the iterative recommender improvement pipeline while keeping deterministic guardrails and persistent state under programmatic control.

Result: AutoLR was built and deployed for DASHEN, integrating council-based adversarial review, a deterministic selector for trial allocation, and a layered knowledge base; LLMs handle semantic reasoning and code generation while deterministic components manage execution, metrics extraction, and guardrails. The abstract claims improved coordination of the research-to-launch workflow, though no quantitative metrics are provided in the abstract.

Conclusion: The paper presents a hybrid human+LLM+controller system that automates and coordinates complex, long-running recommender development cycles. Key innovations are the multi-expert council, deterministic evidence-weighted selector, and layered knowledge fusion; however, the abstract lacks quantitative evaluation and details needed to judge generality, robustness, and cost.

Abstract: Improving an industrial recommender is an iterative research-and-engineering process rather than a direct path from idea to deployment. In \textbf{DASHEN, NetEase's gaming-community app}, algorithm engineers typically identify promising directions from research papers, technical reports, and prior production experiments; reproduce or adapt the underlying methods; implement them in the production codebase; and evaluate the resulting models through training and offline experiments. Promising candidates are then advanced to online A/B tests, and those demonstrating robust gains are submitted to Launch Review---the internal gate for full-traffic rollout. Large language models (LLMs) can assist with individual stages of this workflow, but the overall process remains human-dependent without a harness that can reliably coordinate them across long-running, often multi-day experimental cycles. We present \textbf{AutoLR}, initially built as \textbf{Auto Launch Review} and later extended upstream into an autonomous research-to-launch harness. AutoLR combines three system mechanisms: a \textbf{multi-expert council} that debates and adversarially reviews proposals; a \textbf{deterministic evidence-weighted exploration--exploitation selector} that allocates a limited trial budget across candidate directions and uses Council reranking; and a layered knowledge system that combines external research, production-system knowledge, and DASHEN-specific domain knowledge---such as game communities, player characteristics, and content-interaction patterns---with posterior evidence from configurations, patches, logs, failures, and offline outcomes. LLM agents perform semantic reasoning and code generation, while deterministic controllers retain authority over execution, metric extraction, guardrails, and persistent state transitions.

</details>


### [89] [MARLA: A Conceptual Scaffold for Regulatory Learning under the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04877)
*Alessio Buscemi,Tom Deckenbrunnen,Imane Hmiddou,Marco Billi,Livio Rubino,Silvia Rizzuto Ferruzza,Daniele Pagani,Antonino Rotolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces MARLA, a five-stage scaffold (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt) to organise regulatory learning across Local/National/European levels for implementing the EU AI Act; illustrated with case studies to show how it helps translate implementation evidence into governance knowledge.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the gap between evidence produced during AI Act implementation and its translation into governance/legal knowledge, given differing professional worlds of evidence producers and users, to enable consistent interpretation and adaptive oversight.

Method: The authors develop MARLA, a conceptual scaffold comprising five stages (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt), and illustrate its use via two piloted case studies and a prospective National-to-European example, focusing on documenting regulatory learning at Local, National and European governance levels.

Result: MARLA offers a non-prescriptive shared vocabulary and documentable outputs for the first three stages, demonstrated by case studies, showing potential to improve cross-level regulatory learning and support the EU AI Act's aims.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that effective implementation of the EU AI Act requires structured regulatory learning bridging technical and legal domains; MARLA provides a five-stage, multi-level scaffold to translate implementation evidence into governance knowledge, facilitating consistency, oversight, and adaptation.

Abstract: The EU AI Act positions regulation as part of the infrastructure for safe, trustworthy and market-ready innovation. Realising this ambition requires regulatory learning: the evidence generated during implementation must be translated into governance and legal knowledge that supports consistent interpretation, effective oversight, and adaptation as technologies evolve. Yet the actors who produce this evidence and those who rely on it operate in different professional worlds. This paper proposes MARLA (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt), a conceptual scaffold organising regulatory learning as a five-stage cycle centred on the implementation of legal requirements into socio-technical practices, situated at the Local, National and European levels of the AI Act's governance architecture. Deliberately non-prescriptive, MARLA gives technical and legal stakeholders a shared vocabulary in which each of the first three stages generates its own documentable form of regulatory learning. We illustrate the scaffold with two piloted case studies and a prospective National-to-European illustration.

</details>


### [90] [Reinforcement Learning for Sequential Solar PV Policy Design under Uncertainty: An Agent-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04880)
*Iias Faiud,Jonaid Shianifar,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TL;DR：本文用RL联动随机ABM，在16年期内学得动态光伏激励策略，揭示清晰的采纳—成本权衡并优于静态策略，显示RL在不确定环境下设计自适应政策的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是要在存在不确定性和行为异质性的现实条件下，权衡光伏装机增长与公共财政支出，从而设计出既有效又财政可持续的激励政策。

Method: 方法是把光伏补贴设计建模为一个序贯决策问题：在16年跨期内，政策制定者智能体每年选择资本补贴、优惠贷款利率和上网电价等激励；用随机化的ABM模拟年度装机采纳；以标量化奖励（包含采纳与费用权重）描述目标；用PPO、SAC、TD3三种RL算法训练策略并在随机仿真下评估。

Result: 结果展示了稳健的采纳—成本权衡曲线：最大采纳（TD3,w_cost=0.5）约4145户、成本41.73M欧元；最低成本（PPO,w_cost=2.0）7.27M欧元、2682户；平衡策略（PPO,w_cost=1.6）3495户、22.47M欧元。跨算法保持一致的模式，且RL能探索比静态基线更广的策略空间。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：将强化学习与随机代理基础模型结合，可在不确定性和异质行为下学得逐年可调整的光伏激励政策，揭示清晰的采纳—成本权衡并优于静态基线策略，表明RL是一种有前景的自适应政策设计工具。

Abstract: Designing effective and fiscally sustainable policies for solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption requires balancing adoption gains against public expenditure under uncertainty and heterogeneous decision-making. This study formulates PV policy design as a sequential decision problem and integrates reinforcement learning (RL) with a stochastic agent-based model (ABM) that simulates yearly solar PV adoption under uncertainty. A policymaker agent selects annual incentives, including capital grants, subsidised loan rates, and feed-in tariffs, over a 16-year horizon. Adoption--cost trade-offs are explored by varying policy preferences within a scalarised reward framework. Policies are learned using PPO, SAC, and TD3 and evaluated under stochastic simulation. The results show that this approach produces a clear trade-off structure: the highest-adoption policy (TD3, $w_{\text{cost}}=0.5$) achieves approximately 4,145 adopters at a cost of EUR 41.73 million, while the lowest-cost policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=2.0$) reduces expenditure to EUR 7.27 million with 2,682 adopters. The balanced policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=1.6$) achieves 3,495 adopters at a cost of EUR 22.47 million. Across algorithms, consistent trade-off patterns are observed, indicating robustness of the adoption--cost relationship. Compared with static baseline policies, the RL framework explores a broader range of policy configurations. These findings demonstrate the potential of RL as a flexible tool for adaptive policy design under uncertainty.

</details>


### [91] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文批判性综述了截至2026-08-31的证据：模型在扩展动作接口方面进展明显，但并未形成可信的、不需人类持续干预的自主代理。提出“有正当理由的委托”原则与针对性研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清关于“自治”的常见混淆，评估哪些方面已有可信进展，提出以证据为基的委托准则与后续研究优先级，以降低把未经充分验证的权限赋予模型带来的风险。

Method: 系统性综述与概念框架化：收集并合成截至2026-08-31的主要研究与技术规范，按委托权威、时间持久性、环境耦合分类证据，区分模型、束缚件（harness）与环境三层分析。

Result: 该综述将证据沿“委托权威、时间持久性、环境耦合”三轴组织，并分别分析模型、束缚件（harness）与环境。结论是：行动接口扩展（模型能调用工具、操作界面、控制外设等）证据充分；而稳健完成任务、恢复失败、获得授权与独立验证的证据不足。作者提出“有正当理由的委托”作为规范性启发式，并给出耦合模型—束缚件评估、基于能力的权限、持久状态管理、跨代理问责与分阶段物理验证的研究议程。

Conclusion: 不要把系统集成、保持状态与模型能力等同于自治；扩大模型行动权限仅在有溯源、界定权限、故障检测与安全恢复证据时才可。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [92] [Compact-Memory LLM Agents via Online Max-Member Clustering and Atom-Aware Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04915)
*Jiahe Geng,Jinpeng Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSM-full is a compact-memory pipeline optimized for the 2k–5k token prompt regime; its key innovations (cosine-gated max-member merge and grouped packing) materially improve quality per token compared to streaming-clustered baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon LLM use cases have tight prompt budgets (latency, cost, context length) so full-context prompting is impractical; need memory designs that maximize quality per token in the compact-memory regime.

Method: Online clustered-memory pipeline combining (1) a cosine-gated max-member merge write rule (merges incoming atoms into cluster members based on cosine threshold, keeping the most representative member) and (2) an atom-aware grouped context packer (packs related atoms together rather than flat concatenation) to produce compact, high-quality context for LLM prompting.

Result: RSM-full, an online clustered-memory pipeline with a cosine-gated max-member merge rule and atom-aware grouped context packer, achieves strong quality–token Pareto performance: on AMA-Bench it attains 83% of Full-Context quality at 32% token cost (4k budget) and outperforms Online K-Means by +3.5–6.0 pp (p<.001) across ~2.6k–~5k; ablations show most gains from the merge rule and grouped packer. On RealMem, it improves over several baselines (e.g., +0.69 pp vs Budget-RAG, p=.006) and is competitive with BM25-RAG.

Conclusion: Under tight prompt budgets (~2k–5k tokens), compact-memory performance is mainly determined by how streaming memories are merged and how retrieved atoms are packed; RSM-full defines a strong Pareto point in that regime, though higher-token baselines dominate outside it.

Abstract: Many long-horizon LLM deployments face tight prompt budgets: latency, cost, and context limits make full-context prompting impractical as interaction length grows. The key question is then not raw recall alone, but which memory design gives the best quality--token trade-off in the compact-memory regime. We present \textbf{RSM-full}, an online clustered-memory pipeline designed for a strong quality--token Pareto point.
  RSM-full combines two design choices: a cosine-gated \emph{max-member merge} write rule and an atom-aware grouped context packer. On AMA-Bench, our primary compact-memory benchmark, it reaches $83%$ of Full-Context quality at $32%$ of the token cost at a $4$k budget; under four-seed averaging it beats the closest streaming-clustered baseline (Online K-Means) by $+3.5$--$6.0$,pp ($p{<}.001$) across the whole ${\sim}2.6$k--${\sim}5$k regime. Three-seed ablations show most of this gain comes from the merge rule ($+5.7$,pp over Online K-Means and matched-$τ$ DP-means) and the grouped packer ($+5.0$,pp over flat concatenation).
  The pattern reproduces on RealMem, an independent long-horizon persona-memory benchmark: RSM-full improves on Budget-RAG ($+0.69$,pp, $p{=}.006$), is on par with BM25-RAG (paired $Δ{=}{+}0.27$,pp, $p{=}.47$; we do \emph{not} claim BM25 equivalence in the equivalence-test sense), and significantly outperforms Streaming-Proto ($+2.97$,pp) and the closest reproduced 2025 agentic-memory baseline A-MEM ($+1.65$,pp, $p{<}.001$). Across benchmarks the message is consistent: under tight budgets, compact-memory performance is driven mainly by how streaming memories are merged and how retrieved content is assembled.
  Overall, RSM-full is most useful when answeroughly $2k$--$5k$ prompt tokens, where itdefines a strong compact-memory Pareto point; higher-token baselines remain stronger outside this regime.

</details>


### [93] [Artificial Intelligence in Equity and Crypto Markets: Progress, Profitability Evidence, and the Limits of Automated Investing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04917)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 截至2026-08-31的公开研究显示：AI在投资流程上有真实但主要是上游的进展；没有证据表明任一通用AI架构能稳定地、考虑成本与容量后，在不同市场与周期中实现净超额收益；需要更严格的点时数据、决策对齐评估、联合组合-执行测试与前瞻性验证以提高主张可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术能力并不等同于投资盈利；需要评价AI在真实投资实现路径上的证据强度，明确哪些环节已有可信进展，哪些环节证据薄弱，指导研究与实践者在数据、评估和治理方面提高主张可信度。

Method: 采用“alpha-translation chain”框架，从点时信息、信号稳定性、可行头寸、可执行订单到成本后的风险调整回报，系统性地回顾并组织截至2026-08-31的公开研究，覆盖上市股票、ETF、中心化加密现货、永续合约与链上市场；按方法学类（机器学习、时序基础模型、金融语言模型、强化学习、智能体）评估上游预测与下游实现之间的证据链，并识别常见偏差与实施障碍。

Result: 总结发现：上游进展明显（预测、文本处理、组合设计、工作流整合），但下游证据薄弱（净超额收益难以维持）；历史强结果往往伴随预测衰减与修正后的前视性错误；加密资产需分别对现货、永续与链上现金流与执行处理；没有任何通用AI架构被证明能产生持久的跨规制净超额收益；提出提高可信度的必要条件，并强调即使满足也不保证盈利。

Conclusion: 本综述认为，尽管AI在预测、文本处理、投资组合设计和工作流整合方面取得了可见进展，但在可持续的、考虑成本和市场机制后的净超额收益（net alpha）证明方面证据不足；时间污染、筛选偏差、生存偏差、弱基准、实施成本、交易场所机制及容量限制等因素，会阻断从预测到净超额收益的转换；到2026-08-31为止的公开研究未显示任何通用AI架构能实现跨市场、跨周期、容量意识的持续净超额收益；要提高主张可信度，需要点时数据与模型、决策对齐目标、组合-执行联合评估、受控适应、前瞻性测试与匹配授权的治理。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) now supports investment workflows from data and prediction through research, portfolios, execution, and tool use. Technical capability, however, is not evidence of investment profitability. This critical state-of-the-art review examines public research available through 31 August 2026 on listed equities, exchange-traded funds, centralized crypto spot, perpetual futures, and on-chain markets.
