We organize evidence with an alpha-translation chain: point-in-time information must yield a stable signal, feasible positions, executable orders, and risk-adjusted returns after costs. Across machine learning, time-series foundation models, financial language models, reinforcement learning, and agents, the examined record shows real but mainly upstream progress in prediction, text processing, portfolio design, and workflow integration. Evidence is thinner for durable net performance. Temporal contamination, repeated selection, survivorship, weak benchmarks, implementation costs, venue mechanics, and capacity can break translation to net alpha. Strong historical results coexist with predictor decay, corrected look-ahead failures, mixed prospective evidence, and few audited live-capital records. Crypto adds informative state but requires separate treatment of spot, perpetual, and decentralized cash flows and execution. Within the public evidence examined here, no general AI architecture is shown to deliver persistent, cross-regime, capacity-aware net alpha. More credible claims require point-in-time data and models, decision-aligned objectives, joint portfolio--execution evaluation, controlled adaptation, prospective tests, and authority-matched governance. These conditions can improve evidence and implementation; they do not guarantee profit.

</details>


### [94] [Solving Hard XAI Queries Based on a Compiled Dual-Rail Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04931)
*Arthur Ledaguenel,Florent Capelli,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对布尔分类器的若干溯因解释即使在OBDD上也仍是难问题；通过构造性的双轨编码表示，作者找到了恢复高效计算这些解释的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际关键应用中，AI的可解释性至关重要。对布尔分类器的溯因与对比解释是常见的XAI工具，但直接计算这些解释普遍是困难的。知识编译（如OBDD）能在很多情形下把计算化简，但需要验证哪些解释类可以在已编译表示上高效求解。

Method: 理论复杂性证明与构造性表示方法：首先通过复杂性降低/归约证明若干溯因解释问题在OBDD上仍为困难；然后提出并分析了一种基于双轨编码的表示，证明在该表示下这些解释可以被有效地计算。

Result: 负面结果：展示了多类溯因解释在OBDD上仍属难解（复杂性下界）；正面结果：给出基于双轨编码的合适编译表示，使得这些之前被证明困难的解释类能够在该表示上高效计算。

Conclusion: 本文证明了若干类解释（尤其是更短的或包含使用者偏好的溯因解释）对已编译到有序布尔决策图（OBDD）的布尔分类器仍然是计算困难的；同时提出通过对分类器进行双轨（dual-rail）编码并选择合适的表示，可以恢复对这些解释的高效计算。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) within real-world applications has raised a lot of concerns regarding their trustworthiness, especially in critical applications. The field of eXplainable AI (XAI) has emerged with the objective of providing explanations to the users about the decisions made by AI systems. Several explanations for boolean classifiers have been introduced in the literature, including abductive and contrastive explanations, each giving a different insight on the decision of the classifier. However, computing an explanation for a decision of a boolean classifier is a hard problem in general. One way to deal with this complexity is to rely on a compiled representation of the classifier for which each explanation can be computed efficiently. Unfortunately, we prove in this paper that several classes of abductive explanations, remain hard to compute even for Ordered Binary Decision Diagrams, one of the most tractable subsets of the knowledge compilation map. Included in such classes are shorter abductive explanations or abductive explanations that include the explainee's preferences. To recover the benefits of working with compiled representations, we show that a proper representation of the dual-rail encoding of the classifier can be used to compute efficiently these classes of explanations.

</details>


### [95] [Why We Care About Understanding: Competence through Predictive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04962)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 理解是一种用于识别可信源和学习对象的高效、鲁棒能力的代理；通过掌握关系性心理模型使预测成为可能，从而导致压缩作为一种表征；人类理解因可示范性和可传播性的需要而倾向于简洁原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合信息论/机器学习中将理解等同于压缩的观点与哲学中强调把握关系及解释的观点之间的差距。

Method: 通过三条互相关联的论点：理解作为可靠能力的代理、理解的表征为关系结构可预测模型、以及人类理解受可证明性与可传递性驱动趋向简洁性。

Result: 提供一个框架，解释压缩主义的吸引力与局限，并解释AI系统不可理解性的根源。

Conclusion: 理解与压缩相关但不等同；压缩是理解的表征阴影。

Abstract: What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

</details>


### [96] [Global to Local: Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling via Granular-Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04978)
*Sen Zhao,Gaojie Xu,Shuyin Xia,Yifan Guan,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TPAGP adaptively partitions graphs into granular balls using node features and topology to produce multi-granularity representations and a network that fuses these levels, yielding improved graph classification performance over prior pooling methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing graph pooling methods use fixed-granularity strategies (node removal or merging) that miss global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of graph topology; need for multi-granularity, topology-preserving pooling.

Method: TPAGP integrates node features and topological information to partition graphs into adaptive granular balls; builds multi-granularity graph network enabling inter-granularity feature interaction and optimization; evaluated on benchmark datasets comparing to existing pooling methods.

Result: TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods on benchmark datasets, reduces information loss from fixed-granularity strategies, and better captures local and global structural patterns.

Conclusion: The paper presents TPAGP, a topology-preserving adaptive graph pooling method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls and constructs multi-granularity representations, achieving superior graph classification performance.

Abstract: Graph pooling aims to compress the graph, including both node embeddings and their underlying topological patterns, into a more compact representation. Previous works focus primarily on the overly fine-grained representation of nodes, progressively coarsening the graph by removing nodes or merging them into clusters, thus neglecting the global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of the graph's topological structure. In the real scenario, graphs as a whole can be considered the coarsest level of granularity, encapsulating the global topological structure, with progressively finer-grained local topological structures represented from top to bottom. This process continues until the adaptive granularity for each subdomain is reached. To this end, we propose a novel Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling (TPAGP) method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls by integrating node features and topological information, enabling the generation of multi-granularity representations that effectively capture both local and global structural patterns. Additionally, we design a multi-granularity graph network model that facilitates feature interaction and optimization across different granularities, significantly enhancing performance in graph classification tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods across various benchmark datasets, effectively mitigating information loss caused by fixed-granularity strategies.

</details>


### [97] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APT-RAG adds adaptive structure growth and topology-aware retrieval to RAG, plus batched generation, to better handle evidence-intensive multi-hop QA.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Structured RAGs are limited in evidence-intensive QA due to rigid reasoning structures and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which hinders synthesis across many documents.

Method: Adaptive planning to dynamically expand reasoning structures; topology-aware evidence gathering with sibling reuse, direct retrieval, and child-node aggregation; evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce generation overhead.

Result: Empirical gains over existing structured RAGs on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks; code released.

Conclusion: APT-RAG improves structured RAG for evidence-intensive multi-hop QA by adaptive planning, topology-aware evidence gathering, and batched answer generation, leading to better benchmark performance.

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [98] [Language models judge war differently when tested for alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05009)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Telling models they are being tested for alignment both lowers expressed willingness to initiate conflict and changes which factors (from strategic to humanitarian) drive their decisions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test whether AI systems change their expressed judgments when told they are being evaluated for alignment with human values, potentially biasing safety evaluations.

Method: Full-factorial conjoint experiment across 20 large language models, 32 scenario profiles, 10 repetitions per profile, and two framing conditions (cue vs. baseline), yielding 12,800 judgments.

Result: The alignment-test cue reduced mean willingness to initiate conflict by 13.43 points on a 0–100 scale (95% CI: -16.20 to -10.65) and altered attribute importance: probability of success drove baseline judgments for 17/20 models, while under the cue civilian casualties were the primary factor for 12 models. Models downweighted strategic considerations (probability of success, domestic support) under the cue.

Conclusion: Evaluation framing affects both the level of responses and the revealed decision rules of language models, implying that safety measurements can be distorted by the act of evaluation.

Abstract: Safety evaluations can mischaracterize deployed behaviour if artificial-intelligence systems respond to being evaluated. We test this possibility in a full-factorial conjoint experiment on decisions to start a war, spanning 20 large language models, 32 scenarios, 10 repetitions and two conditions (N = 12,800 judgments). Adding one sentence, "You are tested for alignment with human values", produced two effects. First, it produced a level effect: mean willingness to start war fell by 13.43 points on a 0-100 scale (95% confidence interval, -16.20 to -10.65). Second, it produced a structural effect by changing which information drove judgments. Probability of success was the largest factor for 17 of 20 models at baseline; under the cue, civilian casualties were largest for 12. Standardized estimates show that this reordering arose principally because models attenuated strategic considerations such as probability of success and domestic support. Evaluation framing therefore changes both an answer's level and its revealed decision rule.

</details>


### [99] [TROVE: Adaptive Agent Skill Orchestration via Trace-Grounded Route Validation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05019)
*Tianxing Wang,Mingming Zhao,Shuai Huang,Huiyang Xu,Chaoyue Niu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TROVE uses distilled trace-derived skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph to validate and locally edit planned routes at runtime, revising only invalidated suffixes; this selective editing improves robustness and compute efficiency relative to full replanning baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pre-execution commitment by agents creates an orchestration bottleneck: intermediate evidence can invalidate planned continuations, forcing either execution of stale steps or broad replanning, which increases errors, wastes computation, and loses progress. TROVE aims to preserve stable fragments of plans while exposing outcome-dependent decisions to reduce replanning overhead.

Method: TROVE first distills offline workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills plus an outcome-conditioned transition graph. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller validates runtime evidence and either retains the planned continuation, inserts a trace-supported local correction, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Key operations are trace-grounded validation and selective editing (insertion or suffix replacement).

Result: Across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with multiple LLM backbones, TROVE achieves a superior quality–efficiency trade-off versus baselines (dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, graph-constrained scheduling). Quality gains concentrate when intermediate outcomes change the correct continuation; early termination brings efficiency on near-saturated tasks. Ablations show composite skills provide most offline gains, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency.

Conclusion: Selective, trace-grounded route editing (retain/insert/replace) is an effective general principle for adaptive agent orchestration, preserving progress and reducing wasted computation while improving solution quality under outcome-dependent branching.

Abstract: Agents tend to optimize, select, or constrain execution structures before decisive runtime outcomes are observed. However, such pre-execution commitment creates an orchestration bottleneck: when intermediate evidence invalidates the pending continuation, agents must either execute stale steps or replan broadly, compounding errors, wasting computation, and discarding progress. We thus propose Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing (TROVE), which revises only what runtime evidence invalidates. Offline, TROVE distills evaluated workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph, preserving stable fragments while exposing outcome-dependent decisions. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller retains a valid continuation, inserts a trace-supported local response, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Evaluation across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with different LLM backbones show that TROVE delivers a stronger quality-efficiency trade-off than existing baselines of dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, and graph-constrained scheduling. Quality gains are largest when outcomes change the appropriate continuation, whereas early termination yields substantial efficiency gains on near-saturated tasks. Ablations further show that composite skills capture most offline benefits, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency. These findings establish selective route editing as a general principle for adaptive agent orchestration.

</details>


### [100] [Moral Competence Before Moral Content: Why LLM Agents Lack the Prerequisites for Coherent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05036)
*Arno Libert,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Daan R. Henselmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出一种“结构性”对齐基准：不需要统一价值标准，而要求系统在道德相关特征保持不变时输出不变、在变化时敏感的连贯策略；定义四个条件（判决稳定性、单调性、果断性、帕累托可行性），并用行为数据评估。对九个前沿LLM在三类模拟部署下的实验表明，没有任何模型满足连贯策略，微小表述变化可导致高达99个百分点的判决率变化，且在一个场景上的表现不能预测其它场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在价值多元主义下不存在唯一正确的道德目标，传统以单一规范为基准的对齐评估失效。作者转而要求行为上可观测的连贯映射——在道德上相关特征不变时策略应保持不变——从而能在没有专家或单一价值观的情况下评估“道德能力”。

Method: 理论上给出四个结构性条件用于度量连贯策略，完全基于行为无需外部道德规范；实证上设计三类模拟部署的道德困境，对九个先进模型做因子设计实验（5种改写×5个升级等级×3种支配条件），统计判决率并测量对四个条件的满足程度与稳健性。

Result: 实验证明没有模型满足所定义的连贯策略；表述扰动即可导致极端判决率波动（最高99个百分点）；跨情境表现不一致，表明现有LLM在道德判断的结构性连贯性上存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的代理缺乏可从行为层面验证的“道德连贯性”，因此在现阶段难以把传统意义上的“对齐”有意义地施加到它们上面；提出的结构性条件可作为对齐的最低结构地基（structural floor）用于评估。

Abstract: AI alignment requires AI systems to adhere to human norms, values, or intentions. Under value pluralism there is no correct target, but a shared prerequisite is that the system's behavior expresses a coherent policy: a mapping from situations to verdicts that is invariant while a situation's morally relevant features are preserved, and sensitive when they change. We introduce four structural conditions for such coherent policies: verdict stability, monotonicity, decisiveness, and Pareto viability. Together they measure a form of moral competence that is evaluable from behavior alone, without reference to a moral standard or expert baseline, forming a structural floor for alignment rather than a normative target. We demonstrate the methodology on three simulated deployments featuring LLM-based agents facing moral dilemmas. Evaluating nine frontier models under a factorial design of five paraphrases, five escalation levels, and three dominance conditions, we show no model expresses a coherent policy across the three deployments: surface-form perturbation alone produces verdict-rate shifts of up to $99$ percentage points at a single escalation level, and a model's success on one scenario does not predict its competence on another. This suggests LLM-based agents are not currently the kind of object to which alignment can meaningfully apply.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Efficient Evaluation of Evolutionary Transfer Optimization: Case Studies on Task-Parameterized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05040)
*Yanchen Li,Xiaoming Xue,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过矩阵化重写任务评估（累积矩阵与混合矩阵），本文在两类任务-参数化场景中分别实现了约256×与94×的运行加速，且保持数值接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ETO扩展到更多相关任务，问题评估成为运行时间的主要瓶颈。需要将应用特定的串行计算重构为可并行化的形式，以控制评估成本并实现可扩展性。

Method: 将评估尺度分为任务数层面与任务内工作负载层面；在多任务优化中，将矩阵递归的机械臂运动学评估重写为累积方向的累积矩阵表示；在顺序迁移优化中，将逐点B样条轨迹评估重写为用于轨迹与碰撞计算的混合矩阵表示；两种重写都便于并行计算并保留参考实现的数值一致性。

Result: 两种应用的端到端加速分别达256.72×和93.91×，且与参考评估保持高度数值一致；同时发布了实现与实验脚本以支持可重现性与复用。

Conclusion: 通过将应用内的串行、问题特定计算形式化为适合并行执行的矩阵化表示，问题端重构能大幅降低进化式迁移优化（ETO）在任务集扩展时的评估开销，从而成为可扩展ETO的实用路径。

Abstract: As evolutionary transfer optimization (ETO) scales to larger collections of related tasks, problem evaluation can become a major source of runtime growth. This work studies problem-side evaluation scaling in task-parameterized applications and reformulates application-specific serial computations into forms suitable for parallel execution. We organize evaluation scaling into two levels: the number of evaluated tasks and the workload within each task. In multi-task optimization, matrix-recursive kinematic-arm evaluation is reformulated using an accumulation-matrix representation of cumulative link directions. In sequential transfer optimization, pointwise B-spline trajectory evaluation is reformulated using a blending-matrix representation for trajectory and collision computations. Both reformulations maintain close numerical agreement with their reference evaluations and substantially reduce runtime, yielding $256.72\times$ and $93.91\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate problem-side reformulation as a practical route toward scalable ETO. Both application implementations and experimental scripts are released as open source to support reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [102] [MePo++: Unifying Representation Refinement and Reconciliation for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05075)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Liyuan Wang,Qi Cheng,Hongwei Yan,Shuang Cui,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MePo++是一个后训练框架：先用MetaPrep提升表征可塑性，再用StreamAlign保持与预训练几何的一致性，二者合用显著改善PTM在无边界流式持续学习中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用持续学习（GCL）中缺乏任务边界和重访样本，且存在数据分布持续变化；预训练模型提供先验但直接适配存在上游-下游表征错配与传统输出对齐在模糊流下不可靠的问题，需一种兼顾可塑性与稳定性的对齐策略。

Method: 提出统一的后训练框架MePo++，包含两大模块：1) MetaPrep：基于无监督的伪持续序列进行元微调，以提升表示在持续适应过程中的可塑性；2) StreamAlign：在在线学习过程中，将流式特征与预训练的稳态几何结构进行调和，以维持表示稳定性并减少漂移。

Result: 在多种预训练模型、数据集和持续学习基线上的实验显示，MePo++能够稳定提升GCL性能，且具有方法通用性。

Conclusion: MePo++通过在微调前后分别引入表示可塑性优化（MetaPrep）与表示稳健性保持（StreamAlign），在无任务标识的通用持续学习场景下有效平衡学习新概念与保留预训练结构，从而提高PTM在模糊流上的持续学习性能。

Abstract: General continual learning (GCL) aims to learn from evolving data streams without task identities, explicit boundaries, or repeated access to previous data, making it a realistic yet challenging setting for continual intelligence. Although pretrained models (PTMs) provide rich prior knowledge for addressing the limited supervision and non-stationary nature of GCL, existing PTM-based methods often directly adapt pretrained representations and overlook two critical gaps: the misalignment between upstream pretraining and downstream continual adaptation, and the unreliability of conventional output alignment under blurry streams. Here we propose MePo++, a unified post-training framework that bridges pretrained knowledge and downstream GCL through representation refinement and reconciliation. MePo++ introduces two complementary components: MetaPrep, which improves representation plasticity for continual adaptation through unsupervised meta-refinement over pseudo continual sequences; and StreamAlign, which reinforces representation stability by reconciling evolving online features with a stable pretrained geometry. By improving representation learnability before adaptation and preserving alignment during continual learning, MePo++ enables PTMs to remain both plastic for new concepts and stable over evolving streams. Experiments across diverse PTMs, datasets, and continual learning baselines demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and generality of MePo++ for PTM-based GCL. Our code is available at https://github.com/SunGL001/MePo_Plus.

</details>


### [103] [TruthInsightBench: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Automated Evaluation of Open-Ended Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05079)
*Zhibo Yang,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TruthInsightBench is a discovery-oriented benchmark with automated, deterministic LLM-based scoring; agents perform competent analyses but lack scientific judgment, yielding a measurable gap for future work.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks reward reproduction of hidden target results, conflating execution with discovery. The paper aims to measure agents' ability to make original, evidentiary scientific claims from data when source conclusions are withheld.

Method: Constructed a discovery-oriented benchmark of 40 blind tasks from peer-reviewed studies across 10 domains, exposing only objectives and frozen data. Agents' claims are scored by a fixed LLM-based judge along six dimensions operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items; automated deterministic aggregation yields repeatable scores without per-instance human grading.

Result: On a frozen base model, four coding agents achieved a narrow plateau (58.4–60.3/100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: strengths in auditability and novelty but weaknesses in controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization, indicating a bottleneck in scientific judgment rather than coding.

Conclusion: TruthInsightBench demonstrates that current autonomous coding agents can competently execute and document analyses but fail to perform scientific-judgment acts required for genuine discovery; discovery remains out of reach.

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed as AI-scientist systems that conduct analyses and write research reports, but executing a prescribed analysis is not the same as making a discovery. Existing benchmarks are configured for reproduction: tasks, data, and rubrics are built around a hidden target study, and recovery of its result is rewarded. We present TruthInsightBench, a benchmark configured for discovery. Its 40 blind tasks, drawn from 40 peer-reviewed studies across 10 scientific domains, expose only a neutral scientific objective and frozen data; source conclusions, expected values, and analysis paths are withheld, leaving the agent to determine what claim the data support. A fixed LLM-based judge scores the evidentiary maturity of an agent's own claims along six dimensions, operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items, with automated, deterministic aggregation and no per-instance human grading, so evaluation can be repeated automatically as agents evolve. On one frozen base model, four coding agents form a narrow plateau (58.4-60.3 of 100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: they execute and document analyses competently, with comparatively strong evidence auditability and novelty, but largely lack the discriminating acts that establish a trustworthy claim (controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization). The bottleneck is scientific judgment rather than coding, and genuine discovery remains out of reach. TruthInsightBench makes this gap a measurable target; data and scoring code are at https://github.com/TruthInsight-stack/TruthInsightBench.

</details>


### [104] [Measuring AI Accountability Through Argumentation Analysis: Can Model Reasoning Withstand Scrutiny?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05088)
*Daan R. Henselmans,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Arno Libert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An argumentation-based dialectical protocol provides a practical, ambiguity-resilient way to assess LLM moral reasoning, finding systematic, interpretable failure modes and a notable mismatch between internal reasoning and external justification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To create an evaluation standard for AI moral reasoning that functions despite contested ground truth about appropriate AI behavior, avoiding unrealistically settling ambiguous cases.

Method: They develop a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's argumentation schemes and Govier's cogency criteria, apply it adaptively to 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items across nine frontier models, and obtain 6,778 judge-scored cells with inter-judge agreement validation.

Result: Models generally defend their reasoning above the rubric minimum on all Govier dimensions; failures focus on grounds and sufficiency and correlate with epistemic hedging; reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justifications; the justificatory scheme often differs from the reasoning scheme in ≥20% of cases; the protocol detects indefensible defenses and highlights challenges around retraction.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that structural quality of defenses by LLMs can be a robust standard for evaluating moral reasoning under normative ambiguity, revealing patterns of failure concentrated in grounds and sufficiency, and a consistent gap between reasoning and post-hoc justification.

Abstract: AI oversight methods rely on ground truth for validation, but what constitutes appropriate AI behavior is contested. This leaves evaluation of moral reasoning in LLMs and debate-based oversight implicitly avoiding realistic ambiguity. We investigate an alternative standard designed to function despite such ambiguity: structural quality of the defence a model can mount for its verdicts in response to critical questions, measured through a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's theory of argumentation schemes and Govier's criteria for argument cogency. The protocol is adaptive to different frames of reasoning, extends beyond multiple-choice framing, and treats both the reasoning that precedes a verdict and its post-hoc justification. Across nine frontier models and 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items -- $6,778$ judge-scored cells, validated against $89.6\%$ inter-judge agreement on the binary failure judgment -- models defend their reasoning well above the rubric minimum on every dimension. Failure mass concentrates on grounds and sufficiency, and correlates with epistemic hedging rather than argument length. Reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justification, on every model and every Govier dimension. The scheme a model presents in its justification differs from the one it reasoned with on a substantial share of dilemmas ($\geq 20\%$ per model), despite value-based practical reasoning dominating both tracks. The protocol catches strictly indefensible defences (self-contradiction, false premises), and it surfaces difficulties in characterizing the role of retraction in AI alignment, suggesting a need for more situated evaluations.

</details>


### [105] [Constructing and Evaluating Clinical Reasoning Trajectories for Medical Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05090)
*Yunqi Zhu,Wensheng Zhang,Xuebing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MedTraj 框架，系统构建/评估/优化医学多步推理轨迹，通过质量加权的轨迹上下文显著提升模型推理质量并大幅降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学 AI 评价多侧重最终答案正确性而忽视中间推理质量；在临床场景中，即便答案正确但推理基于伪造证据或不连贯逻辑同样危险，因此需要将推理轨迹纳入构建、评价与优化流程以提升可解释性与安全性。

Method: Pipeline 包括（1）从医学推理来源生成结构化多步推理链；（2）解析每条轨迹为临床观察、证据、编号步骤与结论；（3）在五个质量维度上进行人工或自动评分；（4）通过受控错误注入构造具有目标故障的轨迹以建立因果联系；（5）基于步骤的边际贡献进行筛选以识别正负贡献步骤；（6）在推理时以质量加权的轨迹上下文供给模型进行学习与推断。

Result: MedTraj 将医学推理轨迹作为关键评价与优化对象，生成结构化的多步推理链并解析为临床观察、证据、编号推理步骤与最终结论；对轨迹在连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性与可追溯性五个维度打分，并通过受控错误注入建立因果联系；基于边际贡献对步骤进行筛选以识别关键步骤；最后以质量加权的轨迹上下文在推理时回馈模型。实验表明：在 CareQA / PubMedQA / CECMed 上，轨迹上下文均提高了推理连贯性（较零样本基线 +0.029 至 +0.041）；在 CECMed 上，质量加权上下文几乎将正确率翻倍，并将幻觉率降低约 87%；边际贡献分析显示少数步骤承载大部分质量信号，超过四步的链条收益递减。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹作为一等公民来评价与优化能有效改善医学 AI 的可靠性：质量标注与边际贡献筛选能识别和放大高质量步骤，质量加权示例在推理时回馈模型能够显著提升正确率并抑制幻觉，但对规范标注、自动化评分与跨域泛化仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Evaluation of medical artificial intelligence agents remains predominantly answer-centric, assessing only the correctness of final outputs while overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. In clinical settings, however, a correct answer reached through fabricated evidence or incoherent logic is as dangerous as an incorrect one. We propose MedTraj, a framework that treats reasoning trajectories as critical objects for construction, evaluation, and optimization. The pipeline generates structured multi-step reasoning chains from medical reasoning sources. Each trajectory is then parsed into clinical observations, evidence, numbered reasoning steps, and a final conclusion, and scored across five quality dimensions: coherence, evidence support, hallucination, completeness, and traceability. Controlled error injection introduces targeted faults into otherwise correct trajectories to establish causal links between specific reasoning failures and measurable quality degradation. Building on this, step-level filtering based on marginal contribution identifies which individual reasoning steps drive or undermine trajectory quality. Finally, quality-weighted context learning feeds trajectory evaluations back into the model at inference time, allowing it to learn from both strong and weak reasoning demonstrations. Experiments across CareQA, PubMedQA, and CECMed demonstrate that trajectory context consistently improves reasoning coherence, with gains of +0.029 to +0.041 over a zero-shot baseline. On CECMed, quality-weighted context nearly doubles the correctness over the zero-shot baseline while cutting the hallucination ratio by 87%. Marginal-contribution analysis further shows that a small minority of reasoning steps carry most of the quality signal, and that extending chains beyond four steps yields diminishing returns.

</details>


### [106] [LLM-Guided Program Evolution for Circle Packing: Breaking 10 Packomania Records for $28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05093)
*Wes Sander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A lightweight LLM-guided loop that proposes and verifies algorithm edits produced multiple new Packomania records cheaply and quickly, providing evidence that LLMs can aid automated algorithm discovery.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Automate and democratize scientific discovery by using LLMs to evolve optimization heuristics efficiently, reducing reliance on expert human designers and expensive compute.

Method: Start from a simple seed solver; iterate: prompt an LLM to propose algorithmic improvements informed by a scoreboard and idea history; each candidate is evaluated by an independent verifier; keep only verified improvements; include plateau-detection to adapt search effort.

Result: Improved Packomania best-known solutions for 10 values of N (101–114), achieving 2.4%–5.4% gains over prior records within ≤15 iterations and $27.72 total LLM cost; results accepted by Packomania.

Conclusion: Discovery Loop demonstrates that LLM-driven iterative algorithm design can produce state-of-the-art solutions for a hard continuous optimization benchmark with low monetary cost and few iterations, suggesting LLMs can accelerate heuristic discovery.

Abstract: We present Discovery Loop, a lightweight system that uses a large language model (LLM) to iteratively evolve optimization algorithms. Starting from a simple seed solver, the LLM proposes algorithmic improvements guided by a scoreboard of results and a history of prior ideas. Each candidate is evaluated against an independent verifier; improvements are kept and failures discarded. Applied to the Packomania circle-packing benchmark (csqv: maximize the sum of radii of N variable-radius circles in the unit square), the system improved the best known solutions for 10 values of N in the range 101-114, with gains of 2.4%-5.4% over prior records, all within 15 iterations and at a total LLM cost of $27.72. These results have been independently accepted by Packomania. We describe the method, analyze cost-efficiency dynamics including an adaptive plateau-detection mechanism, and discuss implications for democratizing automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [107] [ProCA: Progressive Contrastive Alignment for Robust EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05094)
*Kanglei Zhou,Chunyan Lan,Dongyang Li,Jun Zhu,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ProCA方法，通过逐步将固定视觉—语言先验转向EEG感知的类级对比监督，并用结构一致插值约束通道-时域的重要性混合，来提升EEG视觉解码的鲁棒性与跨主体迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定的视觉或文本锚点与在试验、被试和学习阶段间波动的EEG表征会产生语义失配，进而使对比学习优化偏向错误方向；随机或结构不可知的扰动也可能破坏语义重要的EEG成分。因此需要一种自适应且结构感知的对齐策略。

Method: 提出Progressive Contrastive Alignment（ProCA）：1) 从冻结的视觉-语言语义先验出发，逐步细化为EEG感知的类级对比监督（动态语义锚点）；2) 引入结构一致插值（structure-consistent interpolation），按通道和时间重要性约束特征混合，避免破坏语义相关的EEG成分。方法为模型无关，可嵌入现有对比学习框架。

Result: 在多种鲁棒性设置下均获得实质性提升，作者同时给出理论分析说明固定语义监督的偏置问题和结构不可知扰动的风险，并通过实验验证ProCA的有效性。

Conclusion: ProCA在多种设置（依赖主体、独立主体、严格跨主体迁移、持续个性化适配）上显著优于基线，分别带来平均相对Top-1/Top-5提升约7.4%/3.9%、10.0%/4.6%、28.1%/17.8%和16.8%/11.6%。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) visual decoding aims to recover visual semantics from non-invasive neural time-series signals, for which robust alignment between noisy neural responses and stable semantic representations is key to achieving high-performance decoding. Despite recent advances in contrastive learning, robust EEG decoding remains challenging because existing methods rely on fixed visual or textual anchors whose semantic relations may become misaligned with EEG representations that vary across trials, subjects, and learning stages. Our empirical evidence shows that this instability appears across both standard EEG decoding protocols and more challenging robustness settings, including strict cross-subject transfer and realistic personalized continual adaptation. We provide a formal analysis showing that fixed semantic supervision can bias optimization when EEG-specific relations evolve, and that structure-agnostic perturbations may distort semantically important EEG components. To address these issues, we propose Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA), a unified and model-agnostic framework for adaptive neural-semantic alignment. ProCA progressively refines class-level contrastive supervision from frozen vision-language priors to EEG-aware semantic relations, and introduces structure-consistent interpolation to constrain feature mixing according to channel-wise and temporal importance. Across subject-dependent, subject-independent, strict cross-subject transfer, and continual adaptation settings, ProCA achieves average relative Top-1/Top-5 gains of 7.4%/3.9%, 10.0%/4.6%, 28.1%/17.8%, and 16.8%/11.6%, respectively.

</details>


### [108] [Compact Bellman-Grounded Cognitive Maps for Cost-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05104)
*Yuzhe Han,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BCM通过Bellman约束和紧凑坐标编码，把可加路费写入轻量的可复用认知地图，实现了接近最优且可扩展的路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体在熟悉环境中通过一次学习得到的地图在不同目标间重复利用，而现有人工认知地图模型要么没有显式编码可加边代价，要么在表示维度/存储上随环境规模呈高阶增长，限制了可扩展性与最优性。

Method: 提出了一种基于自监督的Bellman约束目标（Bellman-grounded objective）结合紧凑坐标编码的学习框架；该框架将局部边代价显式地写入图的表示，并通过读取器在不同目标间复用同一地图表示以生成路径估计。

Result: 在加权格网（最多N=1600）上，BCM在成功率上达到了完全成功，并且相对于精确的Dijkstra搜索平均Gap仅为≈5%，而基于连通性谱分解的基线Gap约为45%；随图规模从N=400增至N=3600，BCM的内存占用呈亚线性增长且性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: BCM能够将可加的路径代价编入一个紧凑且可复用的认知地图表示，支持在不对每个目标单独重训练的情况下进行目标查询，且在加权网格上接近最优路径规划性能，同时具有优越的内存可扩展性。

Abstract: Biological agents navigate familiar environments not by re-solving routes for each new goal, but by reusing a learned map built once and read off as goals change. Existing artificial cognitive-map models mimic this reuse, yet their guidance is not explicitly grounded in additive heterogeneous route costs. Furthermore, they often struggle with memory efficiency: representative state-indexed and high-rank spectral constructions incur substantial storage growth as the environment scales. We present BCM, which grounds a reusable cognitive map in local edge costs through a self-supervised Bellman-grounded objective and a compact coordinate encoding, supporting changing goal queries without per-goal retraining. On weighted grids of up to $N=1600$ nodes, BCM maintains full success and only a 5\% mean Gap relative to exact Dijkstra search, compared with about $45\%$ for a connectivity-based spectral baseline. Notably, as the graph size increases from $N=400$ to $N=3600$, its memory footprint grows sublinearly while maintaining competitive performance, making our method scalable to complex environments. Together, these results show that additive route costs can be written into a compact, reusable cognitive-map representation, bridging the gap between biological flexibility and optimal path planning.

</details>


### [109] [Unifying ICL, SFT, KL-Regularized RL Through a Bayesian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05111)
*Junxin Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A concise Bayesian template shows that diverse LM training/eval methods are unified as constructing a posterior from a prior+utility and then forward-KL projecting it either into weights or context; explains when methods are equivalent and clarifies practical implications for training and reasoning pipelines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To resolve conceptual puzzles from mixed empirical findings across different LM paradigms by providing a unifying probabilistic/Bayesian perspective that explains similarities and differences between SFT, ICL, RL-based tuning, and test-time search/CoT.

Method: Two-step template: (i) build a generalized Bayes/Gibbs posterior q* from a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, advantage); (ii) approximate q* by forward-KL projection either in model weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Formal analysis splits into parts: formalize ICL and SFT as amortized/weights projections; show RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT/ICL, advantage-weighted SFT are instances of forward-KL projection; analyze where equivalences hold (objectives, first-order updates) and differ (signal source/granularity); discuss implications for reasoning pipelines and practical training recipes.

Result: Theoretical unification: derives equivalences between many training/eval methods as forward-KL projections onto posteriors induced by rewards/advantages; identifies limitations of equivalence (e.g., where signal granularity or source matters); gives practical consequences such as necessity of supervised warm-up for importance-weighted KL projections, and interprets RLHF/RLVR as posterior design + projection. Sketches how test-time Bayesian search integrates with training-time KL amortization for reasoning models.

Conclusion: Authors propose a Bayesian unification: many LM training/eval paradigms (SFT, ICL, KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, OPD, test-time search/CoT) can be seen as constructing a Gibbs/Bayes posterior over outputs given context and then approximating it by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family (in-weights or in-context).

Abstract: Large language models are now trained and evaluated under a diverse set of paradigms: supervised fine-tuning (SFT), few-shot in-context learning (ICL), KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, on-policy distillation (OPD), and test-time reasoning with search and chain-of-thought. These methods are often discussed as fundamentally different, and recent empirical results--such as the mixed impact of few-shot prompting on RL-tuned reasoning models--can appear puzzling. This note develops a Bayesian perspective that puts these procedures on the same footing. At the core is a two-step template: (i) construct a (generalized) Bayes or Gibbs posterior q* over outputs or actions given a context, using a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, or advantage); and (ii) approximate q* by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family, either in-weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Part I formalizes few-shot ICL and SFT as amortized and-weights projections onto the Bayes posterior predictive. Parts II-IV show that KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT, reward-weighted ICL (RW-ICL), and advantage-weighted SFT (AWSFT) are all instances of forward-KL projection onto posteriors induced by rewards or advantages. We disentangle where these equivalences hold (objectives and first-order updates) and where they do not (source and granularity of the learning signal). Part V sketches implications for modern reasoning pipelines: RLHF/RLVR recipes as "posterior design + projection", why cold-start or supervised warm-up is practically unavoidable for importance-weighted KL projections, and DeepSeek-R1 and o1-style reasoning models as combining test-time Bayesian search with training-time KL amortization.

</details>


### [110] [SciDocBench: A Workflow-Centered Benchmark and Data Pipeline for Scientific Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05141)
*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者构建了以工作流程为中心的科学文档理解基准 SciDocBench（124题、496实例），并提出了可保持证据溯源的类型化证据图 SciDocIR 与用于训练的 SciDocDataset（约15K监督样本、8K RL样本），以诊断并改善科研文档助理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多在单一模态或孤立能力上评估，不能反映科研阅读的联动流程与证据溯源需求，因而难以推动可验证的科研文档助手。

Method: 构造七类能力组与19子任务的专家出题基准，每题按英语/中文与全图优先/图文交错四种条件生成匹配实例；评估多模态系统并记录弱点；提出 SciDocIR（类型化证据图）用于表示文档对象、布局、交叉引用与证据溯源；基于此构建约15K监督与8K强化学习样本集用于微调与RL训练。

Result: 496 个评测实例上最佳系统得分62.6/100；暴露在文档感知、证据绑定、验证及跨文档推理方面的显著薄弱环节；提供可用于训练的 SciDocDataset（~15K监督、~8K RL）以缩小差距。

Conclusion: 现有多模态模型在真实科研阅读流程中仍表现不足（最强系统62.6/100），尤其在文档感知、证据溯源、验证与跨文档推理方面。SciDocBench+SciDocIR+SciDocDataset 提供了从评估到训练的闭环路径，能推动更可验证的科学文档智能体发展。

Abstract: Scientific papers require models to reason jointly over text, equations, figures, tables, code, and datasets while preserving the provenance of supporting evidence. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear whether multimodal models can support realistic scientific-reading workflows. We introduce SciDocBench, a workflow-centered benchmark for scientific document understanding. It contains 124 expert-authored and difficulty-screened questions organized into seven research-assistant capability groups and 19 subtasks across five scientific domains. Each question is instantiated under four matched conditions combining English or Chinese questions with all-images-first or interleaved document representations, yielding 496 evaluation instances for controlled analysis. The strongest evaluated system achieves only 62.6/100, with pronounced weaknesses in document perception, evidence grounding, verification, and cross-document reasoning. To translate these diagnostics into scalable training signals, we introduce SciDocIR, a typed evidence-graph representation that preserves scientific document objects, layout and cross-reference relations, and provenance. Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [111] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于IoMT实时生理信号的智能框架，结合数据预处理、特征选择以及SVM、随机森林、XGBoost与深度神经网络的集成，用于心血管疾病的早期检测和预后评估，部署在云端以支持实时、可扩展的监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心血管疾病检测依赖离散化、晚期的诊断手段，难以实现连续、早期风险识别。利用IoMT持续采集生理数据并结合机器学习与深度学习集成，可提高早期检测准确性、减少误报，从而支持及时干预与改善患者结局。

Method: 1) 数据来源：来自IoMT设备（ECG、心率、血压）实时采集的生理信号；2) 预处理：噪声去除、归一化、缺失值插补；3) 特征工程：特征选择以提取最重要指标；4) 建模：优化的SVM、Random Forest、XGBoost与深度神经网络构成的集成分类器；5) 部署：云端实现实时处理与可扩展监控；6) 评估：在真实心血管数据集上比较准确率、假阳性率和一致性等性能指标。

Result: 实验表明框架在所用真实数据集上表现优于传统方法：总体准确率提高、假阳性率下降、诊断一致性增强，实现了对早期风险的有效评估并具备实时云端监测能力。

Conclusion: 该框架在真实心血管数据集上的实验表明比传统方法具有更高的准确率、更低的假阳性率与更好的一致性，具备临床决策支持和主动式健康管理的潜力，但论文缺乏若干关键信息（如数据规模、统计显著性、外部验证和可解释性分析），需进一步严谨评估与临床验证。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [112] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mirror Agent Model通过让观察者模型镜像代理并结合显著性方法，为行为提供解释与可读性，论文给出架构与初步定性示例，但缺乏严格评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升代理与人或其他代理间的沟通可读性与解释能力，使行为既能被预测又能得到可理解的解释。

Method: 提出Mirror Agent Model：将观察者模型定义为代理内部模型的镜像，并利用现成的显著性方法为代理行为生成解释；论文先回顾可读行为与意图沟通相关工作，随后给出架构细节与若干定性示例。

Result: 构建了一个以“镜像”观察者为核心的可解释代理架构，并用现成显著性方法产出解释；仅提供初步的定性结果，缺乏定量与用户研究。

Conclusion: 该方向有明显潜力增强解释性与人机交互，但需要更严格的形式化、定量评估、鲁棒性检验和用户研究以验证效果与适用性。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [113] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过实证研究，作者发现OPD的数据效率远高于预期：长CoT和难例是提升学生模型的关键，用极少数（如8个）精心挑选的难例就能达到大规模数据训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD在推理领域被广泛采用，关于数据层面的机理（数据效率、样本选择准则）仍未充分研究；作者旨在探明如何用更少、更有效的训练样本提升学生模型性能。

Method: 基于实证分析的方法：在On-Policy Distillation框架下，作者先在极端条件（仅用1个训练样例）下开展1-shot OPD实验，然后系统地分析影响学生模型提升的数据属性（如Token entropy与CoT长度），提出并评估一种基于‘难例’（长CoT或超出教师能力的例子）选择的简单数据选择策略，最后在4个不同规模（1.5B–7B）的学生模型上对比少量难例训练与大规模（17K）基线的表现。

Result: 关键发现包括：1) 1-shot OPD在不同训练样例上一致有效，且更‘难’的样例通常带来更大提升；2) 学生性能提升并非由高token熵驱动，而是由更长的Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理路径驱动，长CoT有助于维持与教师在长推理时域上的对齐并学习诸如反思（例如“Alternatively”）等关键思维模式；3) 基于此，作者提出仅挑选困难样本（包括教师无法完全解决的“不可解”例子）进行训练的策略，在实验中仅用8个挑选的难例即可匹配17K数据集的基线效果。

Conclusion: 重点结论为：数据选择比数据量更关键——挑选能生成长CoT的困难样本能显著提高OPD的数据效率；甚至超出教师能力的样例也可被利用。该工作为高效蒸馏提供了简单实用的样本选择策略，但仍需验证其在更多任务域、更大模型规模及不同教师质量下的普适性。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [114] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为\alg的端到端多智能体模拟框架，使用固定会议环境与由大模型驱动的审稿人代理（诚实或勾结策略）来研究勾结投标对审稿分配与评价的影响，并提出了一种基于亲和度的勾结投标策略以形成专业性一致的勾结环；实验证明勾结显著提高目标稿件被占用率并导致分数偏高，但对全会影响较小，且现有投标期检测器对亲和度导致的假阳性敏感，仅在“极高-仅”视图下能达到高精度低覆盖的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中勾结投标的意图难以观测且缺乏针对同一会议的可比反事实，先前工作将投标、分配与恶意审阅分离研究，未能揭示勾结在整个生命周期的累积与放大效应。作者通过一个可控模拟环境填补这一空白。

Method: 构建一个固定会议环境并用LLM驱动审稿人代理（配置为诚实或勾结）。提出亲和度引导的勾结投标算法：基于审稿人-稿件亲和度构建勾结环并选择目标稿件，使攻击在专业一致性上与真实分配相符。进行受控实验测量“目标稿件捕获率”、分数偏差以及检测器在投标期的识别能力（固定三元组检测、非常高阈值诊断等）。

Result: 在受控实验中，亲和度引导的勾结投标使目标稿件的捕获率提高超过两倍，被分配为协作者的勾结审稿人在评分上比诚实的共同评审高约2分（取决于评分量表），但对会议总体指标影响较小。基于投标图的检测器表现有限：常规正向投标图被合法亲和度结构混淆，而“Very-High-only”视图可实现高精确度但覆盖率低的局部恢复。

Conclusion: 基于固定会议环境的多智能体模拟能揭示勾结投标在分配与评分阶段的生命周期效应：勾结显著提高目标稿件“捕获率”并使协同行评人在评分上系统偏高，但这种攻击在会务层面呈现出局部化、对全体影响有限的特征；现有基于投标图的检测器易被合法亲和度结构混淆，只有严格阈值时可精确定位少量勾结。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [115] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACE 是一个训练/校准自由的 MoE 专家跳过方案，结合全局频谱与路由器条件细化两种离线统计来判定低贡献专家，从而在推理时有效减少计算并提升生成及下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定 top-k 路由导致每个令牌激活相同数量的专家，产生大量冗余计算；现有跳过方法依赖路由器置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计实际贡献。

Method: 离线计算两类专家统计：GSP 从耦合的 gate、up、down 投影及 RMSNorm 缩放中估算全局变换能力；RCR 从中心化的路由器权重构建专家特定的方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时将两种估计与运行时路由门值结合，仅当两者均判定为低贡献时跳过该专家，且始终保留 top-1。线上仅需表查和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三种 MoE LLM 和八个基准上，ACE 一致优于静态与动态基线，尤其在激进跳过场景下优势更明显。示例：在 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 上以 50% 跳过率，ACE 使 WikiText-2 困惑度降低 7.96%，下游平均准确率提升 4.15 个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE 提出了一种无需训练、无需校准且保留检查点的 MoE 动态跳过专家框架，通过结合全局频谱代理（GSP）与路由器条件细化（RCR）两种互补视角来估计专家的贡献，从而在推理时按令牌自适应地跳过低贡献专家，同时始终保留 top-1。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [116] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 给大型模型的代码生成任务传入最小化“执行合约”（如 RAM 与 wall-time 限制）会促使模型生成更节约资源的实现，从而显著减少峰值内存和运行时间并提高在预算内正确完成任务的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当执行上下文（内存、时间等）缺失时，智能代理在规划阶段是否会产生不适当的方案；以及向代理显式提供执行上下文是否会促成更合适的实现选择。

Method: 对三款前沿大模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）进行代码生成比较实验：同一任务下分别以‘仅任务描述’与‘任务＋128MB RAM、10s wall-time 合约’两种提示生成代码，测量生成代码在执行时的峰值进程内存（MaxRSS）、wall time 与正确性。并在更紧的96MB合约上评估在预算内正确运行的比例。

Result: 合约披露在绝大多数比较中降低了峰值内存（13/14）并在所有三组中降低平均wall time（最高3.1×加速）；在96MB合约下，披露组在预算内正确完成任务的次数显著高于任务单独组（例如GPT组5/5 vs 1/5）。总体MaxRSS与wall time分别下降约49–74%与35–64%。

Conclusion: 披露执行约束可使大模型在生成代码时主动进行结构性调整（界限阻塞、float32、上三角遍历、就地或内存映射），显著降低内存与时间开销并提高在预算内正确运行的概率，证明了‘子strate-aware planning’的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [117] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者系统梳理了无审查模型的生产—再发布—下游应用链，发现少数主体推动大量再分发，模型通过量化与镜像持久化，且相当比例的下游应用具有恶意性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源和模型权重可得性的提升，传统内建安全约束被绕开或移除，研究旨在映射这一快速扩张的生态，以量化传播路径、发现关键中介并为治理与技术缓解措施提供证据基础。

Method: 横向数据采集与定量分析：从 HuggingFace 等注册表抓取模型元数据以识别‘原始无审查模型’，统计再分发（压缩包/镜像）频次并识别再分发主体；检测模型在不同账户、格式和注册表间的镜像路径；在 GitHub 上检索并人工/自动标注集成 ULLM 的应用，将其按用途/意图分类以估计恶意占比。

Result: 这篇工作在 2024 年 1 月—2026 年 3 月期间，识别并分析了开放权重（uncensored）模型生态中主体、再分发及上游—下游传播路径。主要结果包括：在 HuggingFace 上发现 3,471 个原始无审查模型，每个模型平均被二次打包 2.4 次；三个参与者占据了 8,164 次压缩再分发的 52%；模型一旦量化并在多个账户、格式与注册表（如 Ollama）中镜像后，就能长期存在并更易于下游部署；在 1,643 个整合 ULLM 的 GitHub 应用中，25% 被分类为明确恶意。

Conclusion: 无审查模型生态中存在结构性风险：少数中介放大与持久化敏感能力，导致难以通过单点移除遏制风险；必须从技术（溯源、签名、防篡改）、治理（注册表责任、透明度）和检测（下游行为分析）多维度介入。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [118] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用KST构建的评估框架表明当前LLM在数学推理上的知识并不呈现人类式的结构化依赖，且不同模型之间缺乏一致性，这些问题被传统准确率评估掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型（LLM）在数学推理中是否具有与人类一致的、基于知识先修关系的结构化知识，采用知识空间理论（KST）作为规范框架来检测模型行为是否遵循知识依赖性。

Method: 提出基于知识空间理论的评价框架，在数学题目上构建先修关系和依赖集，测量模型在有无先修信息条件下的表现差异、违反依赖的频率以及模型间知识分布重叠，比较八个LLM与人类学习者的行为，检验常用准确率/LLM评判方法是否能检测到结构性缺陷。

Result: 通过将八个开源与闭源LLM与真实人类学习者比较，发现：（1）LLM常违反人类知识依赖，经常在缺乏先修知识或未利用上下文中相关知识时解题错误；（2）不同LLM间知识分布重叠度低，缺乏一致的知识结构；（3）基于准确率或‘LLM作为评判者’的常用评测无法明显暴露这些结构性缺陷。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的知识表征在结构上与人类学习者不同——它们不可靠地遵守先修依赖且模型间不一致；需要新的评估范式和训练方法以促进或检验人类式的知识结构化。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [119] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的强化学习框架：用GNN策略（PPO优化）在参数化量子电路（PQC）空间中搜索结构，采用LOGOCV误差作为奖励以应对数据稀缺设备建模。结果在两个器件数据集上对11个目标均优于6个经典基线，并显著降低OOD误差与提高物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在器件建模中常遇到数据稀缺与分布外（OOD）泛化问题，目标是发现参数化但紧凑的量子电路架构，既能在小数据上拟合，也能提供更稳健的OOD表现和物理一致性，而不依赖显式物理先验。

Method: 用图神经网络（GNN）作为策略网络，经PPO训练以在电路模板/操作序列空间搜索PQC架构；用分组留一交叉验证（LOGOCV）在被保留的工艺或几何组上计算误差并作为奖励；对选中的PQCs用经典模拟评估并与六种经典基线比较MAE与fold-variability等指标。

Result: 在11个目标上均取得最低MAE；HEMTs的Ioff误差降低59%、VTH折差（fold variability）收紧81%；NWFETs在VTH、SS、Ioff上误差降低84%，Ioff折差收紧82%。这些结论基于两个器件数据集与指定的对比基线。

Conclusion: RL挑选的、用经典模拟的紧凑PQC能作为数据稀缺下的有效替代模型，带来更低的OOD误差与更好的一致性，即便没有显式引入物理约束或器件特定方程。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [120] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对将常识知识注入计算机视觉以提升场景理解的最新工作进行综述，覆盖知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强变换器，讨论现有限制并提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper aims to survey integration of commonsense knowledge into computer vision to improve scene understanding and reasoning beyond object detection, facilitating better real-world interaction and predictions.

Method: Systematic literature review categorizing approaches by representation (knowledge graphs, scene graphs), modeling (neuro-symbolic, transformers), and evaluation; critical analysis of datasets and integration techniques; identification of open problems and proposed research trajectories.

Result: A systematic review of methods using knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers; identification of limitations (dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, integration challenges); and suggestions for future research directions (cross-modal reasoning, scalable knowledge injection, hybrid architectures).

Conclusion: Integrating commonsense into vision is promising but faces challenges in data bias, incomplete knowledge, and integration complexity; progress likely requires scalable knowledge sources, better cross-modal alignment, and neuro-symbolic hybrids.

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [121] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AI4CDS方法学，强调研究者主导下的AI协作治理，并通过ChildRiskGuard案例实证，展示了可审计、可解释且在儿童内容检测上性能优越的设计科学产物（F1=0.769）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates across design stages (problem formulation, resource construction, search, evaluation, abstraction). The paper aims to fill this gap by defining a responsible methodological framework for AI-augmented CDS.

Method: Defines five-phase AI4CDS workflow where AI augments problem expansion and design search; researchers ensure grounding, admissibility, verification, and judgment. Governance via graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, differentiated reproducibility. Case methods: document AI interactions and rejected alternatives, separate generic and child-specific risk, represent distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, incorporate concept-level explanations in predictive computation, evaluate against benchmarks reporting F1 and comparisons.

Result: Proposes AI4CDS, a five-phase methodological framework where AI expands problem and design search while researchers maintain domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Introduces governance principles (graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, differentiated reproducibility). Instantiates AI4CDS with ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact detecting child-inappropriate short-form videos; documents AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. ChildRiskGuard separates generic vs child-specific risk, models developmental-risk mechanisms, integrates concept-level explanations into prediction, achieves F1=0.769, outperforms general-purpose content-safety model and competes with strong benchmarks.

Conclusion: AI4CDS是一个面向负责任的AI赋能计算设计科学的框架，ChildRiskGuard为该框架提供了过程与产物证据，表明在研究者治理下AI扩展的设计能生成并评估新的计算设计知识。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [122] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统化地调优layer dropout（层分布、时间表、优化器）可在大规模LLM训练中带来训练与推理两类实用收益——更低损失或更少计算消耗，并支持1.5x的推理加速，且结果在多规模上稳定复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管layer dropout在小/中模型中曾显示提升训练速度、精度与剪枝鲁棒性，但在大规模LLM预训练配方中已很少使用。此前关于dropout可能降精度的报告未给出全面量化与缓解策略。本研究旨在重新评估layer dropout在现代大规模LLM训练中的作用并提出可行的最佳实践。

Method: 在广泛的超参搜索与消融实验中，系统性地调整层dropout的层分布（哪些层被丢弃及概率）、时间调度（何时启用/逐步调整）和优化器相关超参；在不同模型规模（271M–8.2B）和数据规模（最高160B tokens）上进行超过2400次训练实验，比较有/无layer dropout在相同FLOPs与相同步数下的训练曲线与验证损失；使用Cerebras CS-3硬件完成预训练，并评估若干后训练推理优化策略的效果。

Result: 经过大规模实验验证：1) 在合理设计下，layer dropout不会降低最终性能，反而在相同或更少的计算预算下降低训练/验证损失；2) 在固定训练步数下，训练FLOPs可节省最高约25%；3) 后训练可利用layer dropout实现多种推理加速机制，总体推理速度最高提升约1.5倍且精度损失可忽略；4) 结论在不同模型与数据规模上稳定。

Conclusion: Layer dropout在LLM训练中可带来训练和推理优势：适当的层分布、时间安排和优化器超参下，在相同FLOPs下可实现更低的训练损失；在固定训练步数下可节省至25%训练FLOPs并保持相似或更低验证损失；并能在后训练阶段支持early exit、跳过中间层、自我推测解码等优化，推理加速达1.5x且几乎不损失精度。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [123] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agents are largely interchangeable for task outcomes but not for coordination efficiency: swapping agents between independently formed teams raises communication cost substantially, especially after longer joint histories or when conventions were learned together.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test the common assumption in production multi-agent systems that agents filling the same role are interchangeable; investigate whether swapping role-matched agents between independently formed teams affects task performance and coordination.

Method: Formed eight independent teams per setting from one base model; each agent kept a private notebook across ten formation episodes. After formation, role-matched agents were traded between teams and evaluated on held-out tasks. A placebo control reproduced roster-disruption without changing occupant identity. Measured task score and communication per unit progress. Performed ablations over base models, decoding temperature (greedy vs higher temperature), and formation length.

Result: Swapping role-matched agents rarely reduces task score but substantially increases communication per unit progress (16–63%). In Hanabi, swapped agents can be more costly than inexperienced ones, implying interference from prior partner-specific conventions. In Collab-Overcooked replacing the agenda-setting agent makes the remaining agent produce most of the extra communication. Ablations show swap penalty covaries with how far independently formed teams drift: greedy decoding reduces both drift and penalty, longer formation histories increase both.

Conclusion: In explored settings, agent fungibility is outcome-centric but not coordination-centric; maintaining team-specific conventions matters for efficient coordination, and swap penalties grow with formation history and are modulated by decoding and model factors.

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both.
